Conclusion: CV-OPD通过对比式视觉约束与差异门控有效解决OPD的视觉捷径问题，增强学生模型对视觉证据的真实依赖与推理表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise. Building on this observation, our quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that OPD, which uses the answer as a hint, has failed to teach students to genuinely seek out and rely on visual evidence. Our theoretical analysis suggests that this failure arises from a shortcut enabled by the lack of visual constraints during distillation. To address the issue, we introduce Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD), which compares teacher distributions induced by positive and negative crops while conditioning on the same student prefix and augments optimization with a margin-based contrastive constraint, which is modulated with discrepancy gating across token and sample level. Specifically, CV-OPD first introduces a negative teacher unrelated to the answer and designs a Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL) that constrains the feature distribution of student by contrasting the positive and negative teachers, correcting how the student exploits visual regions. The CV-OPD introduces a Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG) that uses token-level and sample-level discrepancies to select visually relevant tokens for optimization, directing the student's attention toward visual content. Extensive experiments across a broad range of fine-grained visual benchmarks demonstrate that CV-OPD substantially alleviates shortcut and consistently improves the visual reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs.

</details>


### [69] [ConClick: Element-Wise Contrastive Learning Improves GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13268)
*Ruotian Zhang,Yuhui Zhang,Junzhe Huang,Yu Xin,Hehe Fan,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统评估10个GUI grounding模型，发现错误点击主要落在非目标界面元素（干扰项）而非背景，且模型会重复选中相同干扰项；据此提出对比学习框架ConClick及数据集ConClick-Data，并引入推理时策略ReClick，显著提升grounding准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding是GUI智能体的基础能力，但现有grounding模型错误率较高，频繁导致下游任务失败。作者通过系统评估10个模型、5个数据集，识别出一个主要错误模式：大多数错误点击命中非目标界面元素而非背景，且不同模型反复选择相同的干扰项，说明模型缺乏区分目标与"似是而非"干扰项的能力。

Method: 1) 系统评估10个grounding模型在5个数据集上的表现并归纳错误模式；2) 提出ConClick对比学习框架，使模型学会区分目标元素与合理干扰项；3) 构建ConClick-Data数据集，包含4,292个具有挑战性的target-distractor配对；4) 采用互补的listwise排序目标与pairwise margin目标联合训练；5) 提出推理时策略ReClick，回访潜在区域并在竞争候选间比较预测。

Result: ConClick将Qwen3-VL-8B的grounding准确率在ScreenSpot-Pro上从54.71%提升至66.03%，在MMBench-GUI L2上从83.72%提升至86.28%，在OSWorld-G上从63.33%提升至70.20%，优于基于同规模或相近规模骨干网络的既有方法。ReClick在三项基准上平均额外带来2.96个百分点的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 通过错误模式分析、构建对比学习数据集与训练方法以及推理时重访策略，论文为构建更可靠的GUI智能体提供了实证洞见与实用工具，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: GUI grounding, the task of locating the interface element specified by a user instruction, is a fundamental capability of GUI agents. However, existing grounding models remain error-prone, often causing downstream task failures. We systematically evaluate 10 grounding models across five datasets and identify a major error pattern: most incorrect clicks hit non-target interface elements rather than the background, with different models repeatedly selecting the same distractors. Motivated by these findings, we propose ConClick, a contrastive learning framework that teaches models to distinguish the target element from plausible distractors. We construct ConClick-Data, a dataset containing 4,292 challenging target-distractor pairs, and train the model using complementary listwise ranking and pairwise margin objectives. ConClick improves the grounding accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B from 54.71% to 66.03% on ScreenSpot-Pro, from 83.72% to 86.28% on MMBench-GUI L2, and from 63.33% to 70.20% on OSWorld-G, outperforming prior methods built on the same or similarly sized backbones. We further introduce ReClick, an inference-time strategy that revisits promising regions and compares predictions across competing candidates, yielding an additional 2.96 percentage-point improvement in average accuracy across the three benchmarks. Together, our analysis, dataset, and methods provide both empirical insights and practical tools for building more reliable GUI agents. Data and code are available at https://github.com/zhangruotian/ConClick.

</details>


### [70] [OWLET: On-Policy Weighted Learning via Evidence Transfer for Video Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13272)
*Qitan Lv,Ke Fan,Hong Wang,Wen Wu,Yilin Wang,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OWLET，一种证据优先的强化学习方法，通过引入包含全局时序上下文与问题相关局部窗口的更丰富教师视角，为Video-LLM的视频推理训练提供更细粒度的证据监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLM后训练方法（强化学习RL与在线策略蒸馏OPD）未能充分利用视觉证据。RL仅使用轨迹级奖励，将同一响应级优势施加于所有token，忽略不同token对视觉流的依赖程度差异；OPD虽提供token级监督，但缺乏对产生输出的时空表示的直接监督。实验观察到，当模型视频视角结合全局时序上下文与问题相关细节时，推理更准确，这为证据利用提供了训练信号。

Method: OWLET是一种证据优先的RL方法，从更丰富的教师视角（结合均匀全局上下文与问题条件局部窗口，无需更大模型）中获取训练信号。具体包含两个机制：(i) 证据感知信用再分配，通过比较两个视角下的token似然，依据其相对证据支持度重新分配正向的响应级优势；(ii) 时空状态对齐，在验证器接受的响应上对齐两个视角的隐藏状态。

Result: 摘要中未明确给出具体实验结果数据。论文报告了广泛实验中的观察：同一模型在结合全局时序上下文与问题相关细节的视角下推理更准确；对比同一学生生成响应在该视角与均匀采样下，token似然与内部表示发生变化，为证据利用提供训练信号。

Conclusion: OWLET通过构建证据优先的教师视角，并借助证据感知信用再分配与时空状态对齐，弥补了RL粗粒度奖励与OPD缺乏时空表示监督的不足，从而更有效地利用视觉证据提升Video-LLM的视频推理能力。

Abstract: Post-training has become a key stage for eliciting reasoning capabilities in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing post-training methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, they do not fully exploit visual evidence for effective training. RL supervises the policy with a single trajectory-level reward, which is too coarse-grained for video reasoning: the same response-level advantage is applied to every generated token, disregarding that the tokens of a video response are grounded in the visual stream to very different degrees. OPD provides token-level supervision through output-distribution matching, but lacks direct supervision of the spatiotemporal representations that produce those outputs. We observe across extensive experiments that the same model reasons more accurately when its video view combines global temporal context with question-relevant details. Comparing the same student-generated response under this view and uniform sampling reveals changes in token likelihoods and internal representations, providing a training signal for evidence use. Building on this observation, we propose OWLET, an evidence-privileged RL method that derives training signals from a richer teacher view. This view couples uniform global context with question-conditioned local windows, providing visual evidence without requiring a larger model. To better exploit this privileged visual evidence, OWLET introduces (i) \textbf{evidence-aware credit redistribution}, which compares token likelihoods across the two views and redistributes positive response-level advantages according to their relative evidence support; and (ii) \textbf{spatiotemporal state alignment}, which aligns the two views' hidden states on verifier-accepted responses.

</details>


### [71] [QCLR: A Plug-in Robustification Module via Quantization for Contrastive Learning in Self-Supervised Skeleton-Based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13281)
*Nanzhu Chen,Feixiang He,Yunfeng Diao,Duygu Sarikaya,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出QCLR，一种面向自监督骨架动作识别对比学习的即插即用量化鲁棒化模块，通过双层量化与注意力引导的多面量化策略，在保持表征能力的同时提升对抗攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架动作识别中，自监督对比学习方法性能优异，但对对抗攻击脆弱。如何在保持表征能力与分类精度的前提下增强鲁棒性，是该领域亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出即插即用模块QCLR。在编码流程中采用双层量化方案，通过量化中断梯度传播并将微小对抗扰动吸收至量化区间内；同时设计注意力引导的多面量化策略，以缓解不可避免的量化误差、维持表征质量。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，将QCLR集成到一系列主流对比学习模型中，显著提升了对基于梯度的白盒攻击与无梯度黑盒攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了极具竞争力的分类精度。

Conclusion: QCLR作为一种通用即插即用方案，可在不牺牲表征能力与分类性能的情况下有效增强自监督骨架动作识别对比学习的对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Skeleton-based human action recognition has gained increasing attention due to the stability of skeleton data across diverse environments and advances in motion capture technologies. Following the success of supervised learning approaches, self-supervised learning approaches, particularly contrastive learning ones, have achieved impressive performance. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. In this work, we propose a plug-in robustification module via quantization for contrastive learning in self-supervised skeleton-based human action recognition (QCLR), which enhances the robustness of representations against adversarial attacks while preserving their representational capability. Specifically, we employ a dual-level quantization scheme across the encoding pipeline, which breaks the gradient propagation and absorbs small adversarial perturbations within the quantization bins. To mitigate the impact of inevitable quantization error, we further design an attention-guided multi-faceted quantization strategy to maintain the representation quality. Experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that integrating QCLR into a progressive lineage of mainstream contrastive learning models substantially improves robustness against both gradient-based white-box and gradient-free black-box adversarial attacks, while preserving highly competitive classification accuracy.

</details>


### [72] [Decoding Register Tokens Preserves Global Scene Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13299)
*David Jacob Drexlin,Julius Hense,Jonas Dippel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在保留完整空间patch表示的同时，是否应保留非空间register token以提升解码与生成质量。作者基于冻结的register-equipped编码器构建表示自编码器，将4个register token与全部patch token联合自注意力解码。ImageNet-1K上，ViT-B解码器重建FID分别降至0.472（DINOv2）和0.442（DINOv3），相对仅patch基线下降9.5%和5.2%；生成任务中DINOv2模型FID为1.641，相对基线下降9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉Transformer学习包含空间patch token与非空间token的结构化表示，其中register token被证明编码光照、模糊、风格等全局场景属性，但基于patch的图像解码器通常丢弃这些输出，迫使解码器仅从patch恢复图像外观。作者因此探究：在完整空间patch表示已可用的情况下，保留register特征能否进一步改善解码。

Method: 采用表示自编码器（RAEs），使用冻结的register-equipped编码器；解码器通过联合自注意力同时处理4个register token与全部patch token。通过外观探针、成对重建误差分析和固定解码器register替换，分析register可访问的全局图像属性及其对重建像素的影响。生成阶段训练多头注意力池化（MHAP）预测器，从采样patch恢复register特征，保持预训练扩散模型及其样本不变。

Result: ImageNet-1K上，ViT-B解码器平均重建FID：DINOv2为0.472（较仅patch基线降低9.5%），DINOv3为0.442（降低5.2%）；对应PSNR分别为19.73 dB和20.54 dB。生成任务中，DINOv2模型在相同采样patch上平均FID为1.641，较仅patch基线降低9.8%。

Conclusion: 在完整空间patch表示已具备的条件下，保留register特征仍能显著提升图像重建与生成性能，说明register token携带patch所缺失的全局外观信息，值得在解码器与生成流程中加以利用。

Abstract: Pretrained vision transformers learn structured image representations spanning spatial patch tokens and non-spatial tokens. Recent analyses suggest that non-spatial register tokens encode global scene properties such as illumination, blur, and style. Yet patch-based image decoders discard these outputs, leaving the decoder to recover image appearance from patches alone. We investigate whether retaining register features improves decoding when the complete spatial patch representation is already available. We study this question using representation autoencoders (RAEs) with frozen register-equipped encoders. Our decoder processes four register tokens together with all patch tokens through joint self-attention. Appearance probes, paired reconstruction-error analysis, and fixed-decoder register substitutions examine which global image properties are accessible from registers and how their values affect reconstructed pixels. For generation, we train a multi-head attention pooling (MHAP) predictor to recover register features from sampled patches, keeping the pretrained diffusion model and its samples unchanged. On ImageNet-1K, our ViT-B decoders achieve mean reconstruction FIDs of 0.472 with DINOv2 and 0.442 with DINOv3, reductions of 9.5% and 5.2% relative to their respective patch-only baselines. The corresponding PSNRs are 19.73 and 20.54 dB. For generation, our DINOv2 models achieve a mean FID of 1.641, 9.8% below the patch-only baseline on identical sampled patches.

</details>


### [73] [Sonic-AMG: Streaming Human Motion Generation from Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13300)
*Jingcheng Ni,Zekun Li,Zhiyang Dou,Xiaoyan Cong,Ziyao Zeng,Hongyang Du,Randall Balestriero,Taku Komura,Srinath Sridhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sonic-AMG 是首个从空间音频流式生成人体动作的框架，通过对声学内容与体相对声源几何分别编码为 token，结合因果 Transformer 与流头生成连续动作潜变量，并采用 rehearsal training 与 spatial feedback 缓解训练-推理不匹配，在 SAM 数据集上取得最优 FID 与 R-precision，并支持实时在线生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作从完整的声学与空间输入序列离线生成动作，而真实场景中人类是在不知道后续声音的情况下对声音做出反应的，因此离线设定与真实感知过程不符，需要流式（在线）生成方法。

Method: 提出 Sonic-AMG 框架：将声学内容与体相对声源几何分别编码为独立 token；以这些 token 为条件，采用带 flow head 的因果 Transformer 生成连续动作潜变量，再由因果解码器重建动作片段。针对教师强制训练（使用真值动作历史与体相对声源位置）与流式推理（依赖生成动作）之间的不匹配，引入 rehearsal training，将全序列教师强制监督与短自回归自展开预测的监督相结合；并提出 spatial feedback，在 rehearsal 训练与推理中均基于生成根位姿重新计算体相对声源位置，以保持空间条件与生成动作的一致性。

Result: 在 SAM 数据集上，Sonic-AMG 取得当前最优的 FID 和 R-precision；同时支持跨声音事件与声源位置的连贯反应的实时在线生成。

Conclusion: Sonic-AMG 首次实现从空间音频流式生成人体动作，通过 rehearsal training 与 spatial feedback 有效缓解训练-推理不匹配并保持空间条件一致性，在定量指标与实时在线生成能力上均表现优异。

Abstract: Modeling human reactions to spatial audio requires understanding both what is heard and where it comes from. Prior work generates motion offline from complete acoustic and spatial input sequences. Humans, however, react to sounds without knowing what they will hear next. We present Sonic-AMG, to our knowledge the first framework for streaming human motion generation from spatial audio. The model encodes acoustic content and body-relative source geometry as separate tokens. Conditioned on these tokens, a causal transformer with a flow head generates continuous motion latents, from which a causal decoder reconstructs motion segments. Standard teacher-forced training derives motion histories and body-relative source locations from ground-truth motion, whereas streaming inference relies on generated motion for both, resulting in a training-inference mismatch. To reduce this history discrepancy, we introduce rehearsal training, which combines full-sequence teacher-forced supervision with supervised prediction along short autoregressive self-rollouts. To keep spatial conditioning consistent with generated motion, we propose spatial feedback, which recomputes body-relative source locations from generated root poses during both rehearsal training and inference. On SAM, Sonic-AMG achieves state-of-the-art FID and R-precision. It further supports real-time online generation of coherent reactions across sound events and source locations. Project page: https://jingchengni.com/sonic-amg/.

</details>


### [74] [PAGE: Parts-Aware Guidance for Co-Speech Gesture Portrait Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13301)
*Yabiao Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yicheng Xu,Teng Hu,Shengzhe Zhou,Yanxiao Sun,Jiangning Zhang,Ying Tai,Yong Liu,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAGE 是一个骨干无关的扩散生成优化框架，通过训练阶段的部位特定重加权与推理阶段的一致性分类器引导和差分引导，提升说话手势视频的局部细节与音视频对齐，并构建中文基准 CAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的说话手势视频生成方法虽然视觉质量有所提升，但仍存在局部细节退化（如嘴唇、眼睛、手部等关键区域）以及音视频对齐不理想的问题，缺乏针对语义重要区域的监督与推理阶段的纠错机制。

Method: 提出 PAGE 优化框架，包含三部分：(1) Parts-Specific Reweighting (PSR)，训练时自适应增强语义重要区域（嘴唇、眼睛、手部）的监督；(2) Consistency Classifier Guidance (CCG)，推理时用音频条件潜变量分类器识别并修正音视频不一致；(3) Differential Guidance (DG)，分别精修面部与身体运动以避免跨区域干扰。并构建 Chinese Announcer Speech (CAS) 数据集作为首个中文评测基准。

Result: 在 PATS 与 CAS 两个数据集上的大量实验表明，PAGE 在感知质量、手势多样性和音视频对齐方面均达到当前最优性能。CAS 相较 PATS 包含更多身份、视频片段与场景多样性。

Conclusion: PAGE 作为一种通用、骨干无关的优化框架，可在训练目标与去噪轨迹两个层面同时提升局部空间保真度与全局音视频一致性，为扩散式说话手势视频生成提供了有效的改进方案，并通过 CAS 基准支持大规模评测。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture video generation aims to synthesize realistic, audio-driven portrait videos from a single reference image and a speech signal. Despite advances in visual quality, diffusion-based approaches still suffer from degraded local details and imperfect audio-visual alignment. We present PAGE (Parts-Aware GuidancE), a general, backbone-agnostic optimization framework for diffusion-based generation that enhances local spatial fidelity and global audio-visual consistency at both the training-objective and denoising-trajectory levels. During training, we propose Parts-Specific Reweighting (PSR), which adaptively increases supervision in semantically important regions such as lips, eyes, and hands, improving visual fidelity in perceptually critical areas. During inference, we introduce Consistency Classifier Guidance (CCG), an audio-conditioned latent classifier that identifies and corrects audio-visual inconsistencies in the generated video. We further design Differential Guidance (DG), an efficient inference strategy that refines facial and body motion separately while avoiding cross-region interference. To enable large-scale evaluation, we construct the Chinese Announcer Speech (CAS) dataset, the first comprehensive Chinese benchmark for co-speech gesture portrait video generation. Compared with the widely used PATS dataset, CAS includes substantially more identities, video clips, and scene diversity. Extensive experiments on both PATS and CAS demonstrate that PAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance in perceptual quality, gesture diversity, and audio-visual alignment. Project page: https://aigc-explorer.github.io/PAGE/.

</details>


### [75] [NovaSplat: Model-Free 6D Object Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting Onboarding and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13309)
*Georgios Floros,Evangelos Sartinas,Orestis Vangelis,Maria Pateraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NovaSplat：从智能手机视频训练3DGS模型，实现无CAD模型的新物体6D位姿估计，超越现有无模型方法，并在家用物体基准上超过基于模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无CAD模型的新物体6D位姿估计长期落后于基于模型的方法，因为无模型方法需在注册阶段从零构建物体表征。本文旨在弥合这一性能差距。

Method: 从注册阶段拍摄的短智能手机视频训练3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）模型：该模型既作为检测与粗位姿估计的模板来源，又作为位姿精化的可微骨干。精化器在无梯度奇异的连续旋转参数化下，结合光度与几何损失优化物体到相机的位姿。另提出NovaSplat-Lite，一种加速3.1倍且精度损失可忽略的推理变体。

Result: 在家用物体基准上超越所有基于模型的方法；在含无纹理与高光表面的工业基准上优于所有无模型竞争者，并显著缩小与基于模型方法的差距。NovaSplat-Lite推理速度提升3.1倍，精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 利用3DGS作为统一模板与可微优化骨干，可有效解决无模型6D位姿估计的表征与精化难题，使无模型方法达到甚至超越部分基于模型方法，并在工业复杂表面场景中展现优势。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of novel objects without CAD models remains an open challenge: model-free methods must build the object representation from scratch during onboarding and have long trailed their model-based counterparts. We present NovaSplat, a model-free pipeline that closes this gap. From a short smartphone video captured at onboarding, we train a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model that serves both as a template source for detection and coarse pose estimation and as a differentiable backbone for pose refinement. Our refiner optimizes the object-to-camera pose with combined photometric and geometric losses under a continuous rotation parametrization free of gradient singularities. On a household-object benchmark, NovaSplat surpasses all model-based methods. On a challenging industrial benchmark with textureless and specular surfaces, it outperforms all model-free competitors while substantially narrowing the gap to model-based ones. We further introduce NovaSplat-Lite, an inference variant that is $3.1\times$ faster at negligible accuracy cost. Code will be made available at \href{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}.

</details>


### [76] [LETHE: Defending against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Generation via Value Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13333)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LETHE，通过破坏I2V模型读取参考图的机制实现高效防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到视频扩散模型可将单张参考图生成高质量、主体一致的视频，存在未经同意伪造视频的滥用风险。主动添加对抗扰动是防护思路，但现有方法对现代I2V模型"后续帧如何读取参考图"的机制理解不足，在常规扰动预算下防护效果有限。

Method: 通过凸几何分析，理论上识别出参考"值向量"(value vectors)的散布(spread)是支配读取过程的关键因素，并实证发现即使轻微收缩该散布也会严重劣化生成视频。据此提出LETHE，直接扰乱读取过程，包含两个互补目标：值坍缩(value collapse)目标驱使参考值向量趋于均匀；注意力吸引(attention attraction)目标将后续帧拉向这些坍缩的token以放大破坏。另构建两个以人为中心的I2V防护基准。

Result: 在多种现代I2V模型上做大量实验，验证LETHE达到最先进防护性能，并具备通用性(versatility)。

Conclusion: 揭示I2V模型读取参考图的机制在于值向量散布，据此设计的LETHE扰动在常规预算下实现强效、通用的主动防护。

Abstract: Image-to-video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single reference image into high-quality, subject-consistent videos, posing threats of misuse as anyone can turn personal photos into fabricated videos without consent. A promising solution is to proactively add an adversarial perturbation to the image before release, such that videos generated from it would be unfaithful or visually corrupted. However, due to an insufficient understanding of what governs how subsequent frames read the reference image in modern I2V models, existing protections struggle to offer strong protection under common perturbation budgets. In this paper, we approach the problem by exploring this underlying reading mechanism. Through a convex geometric analysis, we theoretically identify the spread of the reference value vectors as a key factor governing this reading process, and empirically find that even a mild contraction of this spread can severely degrade the generated video, making it an efficient lever for protection. Guided by this insight, we propose LETHE, a novel protection method that directly disrupts this reading process. LETHE pairs two complementary objectives, where a value collapse objective drives the reference value vectors toward uniformity, while an attention attraction objective draws subsequent frames toward these collapsed tokens to amplify corruption. We further construct two human-centric I2V protection benchmarks and conduct extensive experiments with multiple modern I2V models, which demonstrate the state-of-the-art protection performance and versatility of LETHE.

</details>


### [77] [Exploring Attention Sinks in Text-to-Image Transformers from Diffusion and Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13337)
*Binglei Li,Mengping Yang,Zhiyu Tan,Wenxu Qian,Junping Zhang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了文本到图像（T2I）Transformer中的"注意力汇"（attention sinks）现象，发现其在双向扩散模型和因果自回归模型中均存在但模式不同，且具有架构、推理阶段和组件依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力汇（持续获得与其语义内容不相称的过度注意力权重的token或位置）在大型语言模型中已被广泛研究，但在文本到图像Transformer中的行为与功能意义尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，更好地理解T2I Transformer如何分配和路由注意力。

Method: 作者跨双向扩散模型与因果自回归模型，对T2I Transformer的注意力汇展开探索，具体考察其是否存在、在何处出现以及如何功能性地影响生成。方法包括系统性的注意力分析与针对性干预（扰动汇相关的头和token位置），并引入register token以重新分配注意力汇。

Result: 注意力汇在双向扩散和自回归模型中均出现但模式不同：自回归模型集中于首个token和全局尺度token，双向模型则在模态边界附近、文本区域内部及空间角落形成汇。这些模式随生成阶段变化，并局限于稀疏的层和头子集。扰动汇排序的头比非汇对比产生更大的平均变化，而部分汇位置扰动对双向模型分数影响有限。引入register token后，多种配置可保持或略微提升生成性能。

Conclusion: 注意力汇是架构依赖、推理阶段依赖和组件依赖的现象，为T2I Transformer的机制分析和有原则的注意力设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Attention sinks are tokens or positions that consistently receive disproportionate attention mass, often beyond what their semantic content would suggest. Despite extensive study in large language models, their behavior and functional relevance in text-to-image (T2I) Transformers remain largely unexplored. To fill this gap and better understand how text-to-image Transformers allocate and route attention, we explore this phenomenon of text-to-image transformers across bidirectional diffusion models and causal autoregressive models. Specifically, we explore whether attention sinks exist, where they emerge, and how they functionally influence generation. Through systematic attention analysis and targeted interventions, we identify that attention sinks emerge in both bidirectional diffusion and autoregressive models but exhibit distinct patterns. The autoregressive model concentrates attention on the first and global-scale tokens, whereas the bidirectional models develop sinks near modality boundaries, within text regions, and at spatial corners. Moreover, these patterns vary across generation stages and are localized to a sparse subset of layers and heads. To assess their functional relevance, we perturb sink-related heads and token positions. It turns out that perturbing sink-ranked heads produces larger average changes than non-sink comparisons, while some sink-position perturbations have limited effects on bidirectional model scores. We further introduce register tokens to redistribute attention sinks and find that several configurations preserve or modestly improve generation performance. Overall, attention sinks are architecture-, inference-stage-, and component-dependent phenomena, providing a basis for mechanistic analysis and principled attention design in text-to-image transformers.

</details>


### [78] [S-Space: Exploring Spatial Workspace in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13527)
*Zhiyuan Shi,Xiang Li,Xingzhuo Guo,Shangchen Miao,Weiliang Chen,Qiyu Dai,Jinshan Ren,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu,Mingsheng Long,Jialong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现多模态语言模型中存在一个线性表示子空间 S-Space，其中对象 token 的表示沿水平、垂直和距离三个轴编码连续坐标，并证明它作为模型的内部空间工作区，支撑空间感知、推理与言语输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型展现出颇具潜力的空间推理能力，但其内部支持机制仍缺乏理解。作者希望揭示支撑空间推理的内部表示结构，并借此诊断空间推理失败的根源。

Method: 通过对对象 token 激活关于空间答案分数求导，识别出水平、垂直、距离三个坐标轴；结合因果干预（改变空间判断但基本不影响非空间回答）、视角变换下的内部推理分析，以及感知—计算分解（perception-computation decomposition）和方向词读出等诊断手段。

Result: S-Space 是内在而非由特定数据或提示诱导的，在非空间问题下仍可读出；感知将对象位置编码进该空间以支持准确空间判断，内部推理可对其进行视角变换，言语回答选择性地访问该空间。S-Space 还能为未见实体和观察者分配位置，并连接物理方向与抽象语言含义。方向词读出揭示基本方位与图像相对参考系之间存在系统性耦合；感知—计算分解暴露关键瓶颈：模型能够形成有能力的空间表示，却在跨视角变化的可靠推理所需的空间变换上表现不足。

Conclusion: S-Space 可作为多模态语言模型空间推理的诊断工具，既揭示了大规模语言训练带来的能力，也暴露其局限，尤其是空间变换环节的瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal language models (MLLMs) exhibit promising spatial reasoning capabilities, yet the internal mechanisms supporting them remain poorly understood. We uncover S-Space, a linear representation subspace in which object-token representations encode continuous coordinates along horizontal, vertical, and distance axes. Although we identify these axes by differentiating spatial-answer scores with respect to object-token activations, S-Space is intrinsic rather than induced by specific data or prompts, remaining readable under non-spatial questions. We show that S-Space functions as an internal spatial workspace of MLLMs. Perception encodes object locations into it, forming spatial representations that can be directly read out to support accurate spatial judgments. Internal reasoning transforms it for changing viewpoints. Verbal responses selectively access it, evidenced by causal interventions that alter spatial judgments while largely preserving non-spatial responses. Case studies further show that S-Space assigns locations to unseen entities and the observer, and connects physical directions with abstract linguistic meanings, suggesting both the capabilities and limitations of large-scale language training. Finally, we use S-Space as a diagnostic tool for spatial reasoning failures. Direction-word readouts reveal systematic coupling between cardinal and image-relative reference frames. More importantly, a perception-computation decomposition exposes a key bottleneck: MLLMs can form capable spatial representations, yet fall short in the spatial transformations required to reason reliably across viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [79] [VideoAWR: Reinforcing Video Flow Models with Decoupled-Timestep Advantage-Weighted Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13338)
*Zheming Fu,Wenbin Ning,Ruizhe He,Wei Shang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Lianrui Mu,Chang Liu,Lei Wang,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoAWR，通过解耦采样与优化锚点解决视频生成中奖励回归的冷启动与CFG几何爆炸问题，实现单分支无CFG推理且保持CFG语义，提升视觉与运动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于回归的强化学习用于流模型时，需无CFG采样以对齐目标与参考边缘速度。将AWR扩展到视频生成出现冷启动病理：无引导轨迹坍塌到低保真区域，组内奖励差距虽大却不再反映感知质量，使优化目标任意。反之，若用有引导轨迹恢复保真度并保持回归目标绑定有引导分布，在EMA更新下会引发几何引导爆炸。

Method: VideoAWR解耦采样与优化锚点：EMA参考策略产生有引导rollout以恢复奖励可分性，可训练策略则相对于无引导条件锚点进行矫正。理论上证明该解耦目标精确等价：中心化优势在期望上抵消锚点，滤除奖励中性的引导偏移，仅保留与奖励相关的协方差增量。此外引入解耦时间步矫正，对每个rollout在独立采样时间步监督且无偏，提高样本效率。

Result: 在Wan2.1-1.3B与14B上实验表明，优化稳定，且在无CFG与启用CFG两种情形下均获得更优的视觉与运动质量。

Conclusion: 该方法推理时单分支且无CFG，同时保留推理时CFG的原始语义。代码已公开。

Abstract: Regression-based RL for flow models mandates sampling \emph{without} classifier-free guidance (CFG) to align the rectification target with the reference marginal velocity. Extending Advantage-Weighted Rectification (AWR), which dynamically scales this regression target based on reward, to video generation encounters a cold-start pathology: unguided rollouts collapse into low-fidelity regions where intra-group reward gaps remain wide but no longer track perceptual quality, rendering the optimization target arbitrary. Conversely, utilizing guided rollouts to restore fidelity, while keeping the regression target tied to the guided sampling distribution, provably triggers a geometric guidance blow-up under exponential-moving-average (EMA) updates. We propose \textbf{VideoAWR}, which resolves this tension by untying sampling from the optimization anchor. An EMA reference policy generates \emph{guided} rollouts to restore reward separability, while the trainable policy is rectified against the \emph{unguided} conditional anchor. We prove this decoupled objective is mathematically exact: the centered advantage cancels the anchor in expectation, filtering out the reward-neutral guidance offset and retaining only the reward-correlated covariance increment. Consequently, inference runs single-branch and CFG-free, yet inference-time CFG preserves its original semantics. Furthermore, we introduce \emph{Decoupled Timestep Rectification}, supervising each rollout at an independently sampled timestep without bias to improve sample efficiency. Experiments on Wan2.1-1.3B and 14B demonstrate stable optimization and superior visual and motion quality in \emph{both} CFG-free and CFG-enabled regimes. Code is available at \pdfstartlink attr{/Border [0 0 0]} user{/Subtype /Link /A << /S /URI /URI (https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR) >>}% \textcolor{pearDark}{VideoAWR}\pdfendlink.

</details>


### [80] [OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What's Missing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13790)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Ryo Mitsuhashi,Linrong Cai,Xingyu Fu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出 OccludeBench 与 OccludeTrace，系统评估视觉语言模型对图像中被完全遮挡物体的推理能力，发现直接物理线索任务显著难于场景推断任务，并指出瓶颈在于从整图中定位相关投影信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能否根据图像残留的物理线索或场景语境，推断出被完全遮挡的物体，尚缺乏受控、可诊断的评测手段，因此需要构建专项基准并定位推理失败的环节。

Method: 构建人工整理的 OccludeBench 基准，区分需依赖阴影、反射等具体物理线索的"直接"案例与依赖整体场景语境的"推断"案例；评测五个 VLM 并与人类对比；提出四阶段诊断流程 OccludeTrace 定位视觉推理断裂点；尝试扩展推理、工具使用、少样本提示及先补全再验证的干预方法。

Result: 五个 VLM 在直接案例上平均准确率 42.7%，推断案例 62.7%，差距 20.0 个百分点；人类为 63.4% 与 68.5%，无显著差距。OccludeTrace 显示瓶颈出现在从整图投影中恢复相关物体的阶段：失败样本中仅 50% 成功，而答对样本中达 91%。扩展推理、工具使用、少样本提示改进不一致。先补全再验证的干预可将差距降至统计不显著，且对具备图像生成能力的两个模型均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可控、可追踪的诊断框架，揭示 VLM 在处理图像中缺失视觉信息时的具体失效环节，并为通过"生成候选—对照线索验证"的干预策略缩小模型与人类差距提供了可行方向。

Abstract: How well can vision-language models (VLMs) reason about what is missing from an image? We study this question with OccludeBench, a manually curated benchmark for recovering fully occluded objects from the visual evidence that remains. OccludeBench distinguishes direct cases, where the hidden object must be recovered from a specific physical cue such as a shadow or reflection, from inferential cases, where it can be inferred from broader scene context. Across five VLMs, accuracy averages 42.7% on direct cases versus 62.7% on inferential cases, exhibiting a 20.0-point gap. Humans attain higher accuracies of 63.4% versus 68.5% for direct and inferential images, respectively, with no significant gap. To locate this failure, we introduce OccludeTrace, a four-stage diagnostic that isolates where the visual reasoning process breaks down. The bottleneck emerges when models must recover the relevant object from the projection within the full image: they succeed on only 50% of failed examples, compared with 91% of examples they answer correctly. Extended reasoning, tool use, and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent improvements and do not reliably eliminate the gap. In contrast, an inpaint-then-confront intervention, which prompts models to first generate a candidate hidden object and then evaluate that hypothesis against the visible physical cue, reduces the gap to a statistically non-significant difference for both models with image-generation capabilities. Together, our results establish a tractable, controlled diagnostic for isolating how VLMs reason about visual content that is missing from an image.

</details>


### [81] [Why DiT Models Underperform as Representation Learners without Long Skip Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13344)
*Benyuan Meng,Yiliang Zhang,Jin-Wen Wu,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang,Longtao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过从零训练实验发现，DiT 中的长跳跃连接（LSC）能提升扩散特征质量，并揭示其机制：LSC 将扩散噪声直接短路至所需位置，减少主干中的噪声污染，从而改善特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散特征可统一生成与视觉理解，但 DiT 虽生成能力更强，其提取的特征却较弱，威胁生成-理解统一。作者旨在探究并解决 DiT 特征质量差的原因。

Method: 采用受控的从零训练实验，对比含/不含长跳跃连接（LSC）的 DiT 特征质量；并对扩散主干输入（潜图像与扩散噪声）进行细致诊断，分析两类信号在主干中的传播方式。

Result: 带 LSC 的 DiT 产生更好特征。机制：扩散噪声虽以噪声形式出现，却反映扩散轨迹信息；预测噪声变量 v 或 ε 需同时携带图像与噪声信号，而主干同时承载二者会用噪声“污染”图像信号，导致特征质量下降。LSC 可将噪声直接短路到所需位置，使主干中噪声信号更弱，从而提升特征质量。

Conclusion: LSC 并非单纯技巧，而与扩散目标密切相关；该发现为提升 DiT 生成-理解统一能力提供了理论与实证依据。代码见 https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-lsc。

Abstract: Diffusion feature, i.e., intermediate activations extracted from diffusion model backbones, has emerged as a promising approach for unifying generation and vision understanding (discrimination). Despite the advancement of diffusion backbones into Diffusion Transformers (DiT), it has been found in previous literature that DiT models tend to yield weaker features despite their stronger generation capability. This poses a threat to good generation-understanding unification. In this work, we show via controlled from-scratch training that long skip connections (LSCs) affect feature quality: a DiT with LSCs can produce better features. Furthermore, through detailed diagnosis, we reveal the mechanism causing this effect. Specifically, the input to a diffusion backbone contains both the latent image and the diffusion noise, where the diffusion noise, though appearing in the form of noises, actually reflects the information of the diffusion trajectory. To make the final prediction of a noisy variable ($v$ or $ε$), the diffusion backbone needs both signals. However, carrying both signals through the main backbone effectively "pollutes" the image signal with noises, leading to poor feature quality. In comparison, LSCs can shortcut diffusion noises directly to where they are needed, leaving much weaker noise signals in the main backbone, thus improving feature quality. To name the broader impact, this reveals that LSCs are not a mere trick but closely related to the diffusion objective. The code is at https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-lsc.

</details>


### [82] [TextVBench: Evaluating Text Rendering and Preservation in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13412)
*Ruiqiang Zhang,Hengyi Wang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Wang,Zehua Ma,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TextVBench，将视频生成中的文本评测建模为多目标、多实例的时空对应问题，包含 T2V 与 Ref2V 两条评测轨道，并给出长度加权调和分与额外文本诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频生成器能合成逼真场景，但常出现字符渲染错误、遗漏请求文本或跨帧文本变化等失败。逐帧独立 OCR 难以度量这些失败：当场景含多个目标文本与多个含噪 OCR 区域时，评测者需先判定哪个观测对应哪个预期文本，即缺乏目标与观测之间的对应关系建模。

Method: 提出 TextVBench 基准与评测框架，将视觉文本评测形式化为多目标、多实例的时空对应问题。包含两条互补轨道：TextVBench-T2V 衡量提示词指定文本的创建与维持，TextVBench-Ref2V 衡量参考图像文本的保持。二者采用目标条件化的候选选择而非无约束 VLM 转写，支持单目标映射到多个 OCR 区域，并将帧级空间保真与跨帧时间保真分离；用长度加权调和分汇总，并辅以额外文本诊断揭示单一 OCR 分数掩盖的失败模式。基准按受控的文本、布局、载体与运动属性组织。

Result: 在评测的模型运行中，MiniMax-H3 取得 T2V TextVScore 68.32，最强的 Ref2V 分数为 56.51；属性切片显示，随着同时出现的参考文本区域数量增加，性能显著下降。

Conclusion: TextVBench 通过显式建模目标—观测对应关系，并把空间与时间保真度分离，提供了可归因到具体能力边界的视频文本生成评测方案；属性切片可定位多参考文本区域等条件下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern video generators can synthesize convincing scenes while rendering a few characters incorrectly, omitting requested text, or changing it across frames. Measuring these failures is harder than applying OCR independently to each frame: when a scene contains multiple text targets and multiple noisy OCR regions, the evaluator must first determine which observation belongs to which intended text. We introduce TextVBench, a benchmark and evaluation framework that formulates visual-text evaluation as a multi-target, multi-instance spatiotemporal correspondence problem. TextVBench contains two complementary tracks: TextVBench-T2V measures the creation and maintenance of prompt-specified text, while TextVBench-Ref2V measures preservation of text from reference images. Both tracks use target-conditioned candidate selection rather than unconstrained VLM transcription, support one target mapping to multiple OCR regions, and separate frame-level spatial fidelity from cross-frame temporal fidelity. A length-weighted harmonic score summarizes these factors, while extra-text diagnostics expose failure modes hidden by a single OCR score. The benchmark is organized around controlled text, layout, carrier, and motion attributes so that aggregate scores can be traced back to specific capability limits. Across the evaluated model runs, MiniMax-H3 reaches a T2V TextVScore of 68.32, while the strongest Ref2V score is 56.51; attribute slices reveal a pronounced drop as the number of simultaneous reference text regions increases.

</details>


### [83] [SADL: A Size-Adaptive Decision Layer for Chronic Stroke Lesion Segmentation in ISLES 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13422)
*Marek Wodzinski,Gniewosz Drwiega,Wojciech Szymanski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对慢性缺血性卒中T1w病灶分割，构建11成员nnU-Net集成（覆盖5个设计轴），并以加权软图融合+SADL决策层输出结果；在91例站点分离留出集上Dice 0.7322、lesion-F1 0.7461、PR-AUC 0.8430，病灶级提升显著（p=0.024）且主要来自小病灶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性缺血性卒中病灶在T1加权MRI上难以分割：其灰度表现与健康组织重叠，且体积跨越四个数量级，导致体素级与病灶级指标常不一致。论文动机是在这种“外观混淆+极端尺度差异”条件下，同时改善体素级与病灶级分割性能，尤其是小病灶的检出不漏检。

Method: 方法为11成员nnU-Net集成，覆盖五个设计轴：1) 基线Dice+top-K损失；2) 强增强；3) 逐病灶体积损失重加权；4) 随机权重平均（SWA）；5) 采样与架构模块，将队列均衡采样与残差编码器骨干配对。成员输出经加权软图（soft-map）融合后输入调参决策层SADL：估计病灶体积、施加随病灶大小自适应的阈值、按体素数与峰值概率剪枝连通域，并加入“永不空”（never-empty）救援步骤。所有融合权重与分桶参数均在out-of-fold（OOF）预测上拟合，调参不接触独立测试集。

Result: 在站点分离的91例留出集上，用挑战赛官方评测代码在线评分，SADL取得Dice 0.7322、lesion-F1 0.7461、PR-AUC 0.8430。与参考的单集成成员进行配对非参数bootstrap检验，确认lesion-F1提升显著（p=0.024）；提升在小病灶区间最大，救援步骤能召回固定阈值会静默丢弃的病例。

Conclusion: 结论：多轴nnU-Net集成结合OOF调参的软图融合与SADL决策层，可在慢性缺血性卒中T1w病灶分割中同时获得稳健的体素级与病灶级表现；自适应阈值与never-empty救援对小病灶尤为关键，且全流程调参不泄露测试集信息。

Abstract: Chronic ischemic stroke lesions are difficult to segment on T1-weighted MRI. Their appearance overlaps with healthy tissue, and their volumes span four orders of magnitude, so voxel-level and lesion level metrics often disagree. We build an eleven-member nnU-Net ensemble that covers five design axes: a baseline dice + top-K loss, heavy augmentation, per-lesion volume loss re-weighting, stochastic weight averaging, and a sampling-and-architecture block that pairs cohort-balanced sampling with a residual-encoder backbone. Weighted soft-map fusion of the members feeds a tuned decision layer, SADL, that estimates lesion volume, applies an adaptive per-lesion-size threshold, prunes components by voxel count and peak probability, and adds a never-empty rescue. All fusion weights and per-bucket parameters are fitted on out-of-fold (OOF) predictions, so tuning never touches the held-out test set. On a site-disjoint 91-case holdout, SADL reaches Dice 0.7322, lesion-F1 0.7461 and PR-AUC 0.8430 when scored online with the challenge's released evaluation code. A paired non-parametric bootstrap against a reference single ensemble member confirms the lesion-F1 gain at p=0.024, and the gain is largest in the small-lesion regime, where the rescue step recovers cases that a fixed threshold would silently discard.

</details>


### [84] [Auditing VLM Benchmarks from Existing Evaluation Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13548)
*Denis Degtiarev,Anna Gromova,Sergey Katkov,Artem Mikhailin,Safina Mukhamedshina,Albert Nasybullin,Karina Shaikhutdinova,Artem Tuzov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在VLMEvalKit中加入审计层，复用VLM评测已产生的逐题工件，用六个检测器加一次去除视觉输入的纯文本重跑对基准本身进行审计，并以MMStar为例发现20.2%题目图像零增益、17.0%可由排列零假设解释、剩余+3.2pp超额效应集中在数学与科学类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM排行榜将每次评测产生的逐题预测、判分与元数据压缩为单一分数后丢弃，基准质量本身缺乏系统性审计手段。作者希望复用这些既有工件来审计基准，检验图像对题目是否真正不可或缺，以及是否存在可疑题目。

Method: 在VLMEvalKit上构建审计层：六个检测器对保存的输出做后处理，外加一次移除视觉输入的纯文本重跑，输出逐题报告与汇总统计；对核心量给出排列零假设超额、比例区间估计及模型池敏感性。以MMStar（三个模型）为对象，用项目匹配的排列零假设校正图像增益，并与LLM判分/精确字符串匹配两种判分方式对比。

Result: 20.2%题目图像带来的总体准确率增益为零，项目匹配排列零假设解释17.0%，剩余+3.2pp超额（校正排列p=0.001）；超额集中于数学与科学类，粗粒度感知类不存在。第二检测器标记134题所有可解析模型均选择同一非金标准选项，这些候选集中在感知类，与零增益超额分布相反。用精确字符串匹配替换LLM判分结论不变，评分差异均归于一个模型解析器无法读取的自由文本答案。二元yes/no基准上检测器出现校准偏差。

Conclusion: 审计层、预测工件与生成报告随投稿一并发布；该审计方法可揭示基准中图像非必需与可疑选项分布，且对判分方式稳健。但在二元基准上检测器校准失效，构成需要测量并报告的范围边界；确认知识替代视觉证据的机制仍需逐题人工复核。

Abstract: Every evaluation run of a vision-language model (VLM) already produces per-question artifacts (predictions, judged correctness, metadata) that leaderboards reduce to a single score and then discard. We reuse those artifacts to audit the benchmark itself, and add an audit layer to VLMEvalKit: six detectors that run as post-processing over saved outputs, plus one additional text-only rerun with visual inputs removed, emitting a per-question report. The layer reports per-question findings together with aggregate audit statistics; for the headline quantities, it attaches permutation-null excess where applicable, interval estimates for proportions, and sensitivity to the model pool. Auditing MMStar, a benchmark curated specifically so that the image is indispensable, with three models, we find zero aggregate accuracy gain from the image on 20.2% of its questions. An item-matched permutation null accounts for 17.0%, leaving a +3.2 pp excess (corrected permutation p = 0.001). This excess is concentrated in the mathematics and science categories and is absent from coarse perception, a localization consistent with knowledge substituting for visual evidence; confirming that mechanism would require item-level review. A second detector flags 134 questions on which every model that answered in parseable form picked the same non-gold option, and these candidates peak in the perception categories, whereas the zero-gain excess is largest in mathematics and science & technology. Replacing the LLM judge with exact string matching changes no conclusion, and every scoring discrepancy between the two traces to one model whose free-form answers a parser cannot read. On a binary yes/no benchmark the detectors measurably fall out of calibration, a scope boundary that we measure and report. The audit layer, the prediction artifacts and the generated reports accompany this submission.

</details>


### [85] [ELMA: Benchmarking Anaphoric Compositions for Long-Term Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13549)
*Shun Kato,Dixin Yang,Kotaro Amaya,Yuiga Wada,Kazumi Fukuda,Takashi Shibuya,Yuki Mitsufuji,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"指代性组合指令下的长期运动生成"新任务，并构建了带段落级密集标注的长时运动数据集ELMA，用于微调现有运动生成模型以利用长程上下文，从而提升全局物理真实性与语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的人体运动生成研究多聚焦于短视频片段，面对包含指代（anaphora）的多段指令时表现不佳：早期段落建立的约束需在后续时间中持续保持，而当前模型缺乏长程状态建模能力。这一能力对持续运行的数字虚拟人等应用至关重要。

Method: 作者提出新任务——基于指代性组合指令的长期运动生成；为此构建ELMA数据集，通过全自动采集与过滤流程，从in-the-wild视频中提取高质量、连续的30秒片段，并进行保留跨段落依赖的段落级密集标注；随后利用该数据集微调当前最先进的运动生成模型，使其融入长程上下文信息。

Result: 大量实验揭示了现有模型在处理上下文依赖指令时的根本性缺陷；同时表明ELMA能够显著提升全局物理真实性与语义对齐效果，为长期运动合成建立了新的稳健基线。

Conclusion: ELMA数据集与相应微调方案为长期、上下文依赖的人体运动生成提供了有效解决路径，并确立了该新任务下的性能基线，推动数字虚拟人等长时应用的发展。

Abstract: Text-guided long-term human motion generation is essential for applications such as continuously operating digital avatars. Despite recent progress on short clips, existing models struggle with multi-segment instructions that involve anaphora, where constraints established in earlier segments must be preserved over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel task of long-term motion generation from such anaphoric compositions. To mitigate this, we present ELMA, an episodic long-term motion dataset with dense segment-level annotations that preserve cross-segment dependencies. Built via a fully automated collection and filtering pipeline, ELMA extracts high-quality, continuous 30-second clips from in-the-wild videos, capturing the episodic narratives necessary for long-range state modeling. Leveraging this dataset, we fine-tune state-of-the-art motion generation models to incorporate long-range context. Extensive experiments reveal fundamental shortcomings in current models when handling context-dependent instructions. We demonstrate that ELMA enables significant improvements in both global physical realism and semantic alignment, setting a robust new baseline for long-term motion synthesis.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Holistic Scene Layout against View Shifts in Cross-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13553)
*Chao Yang,Songlian Li,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCM与HFIG两个模块，通过建模多方向空间上下文并引入频域高频结构信息，提升跨视角地理定位的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角地理定位受视角变化带来的外观差异与几何畸变影响显著。现有方法多依赖判别性特征学习或显式几何对齐，忽视了人类匹配场景时依赖多个地标空间上下文整合的机制，因而在未知朝向、有限视场等挑战场景下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出空间上下文建模（SCM）模块，沿四个正交方向遍历特征图，并在每个空间位置对方向性表示进行交互建模，聚合互补的方向观测以构建更丰富的场景布局表示；同时引入高频信息引导（HFIG）模块，从频域提取对表观变化不敏感的稳定结构线索（边缘与轮廓），引导模型学习更鲁棒的特征表示。

Result: 在标准跨视角地理定位基准上取得有竞争力的性能，并在未知朝向与有限视场（FoV）等更具挑战性的场景下展现出较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将多方向空间上下文建模与频域高频结构引导相结合，可有效缓解跨视角外观差异与几何畸变问题，为跨视角地理定位提供兼顾精度与鲁棒性的解决思路。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization remains challenging due to drastic appearance variations and geometric distortions caused by viewpoint changes. Existing methods primarily rely on discriminative feature learning or explicit geometric alignment, yet often overlook an important observation: humans tend to match scenes by integrating the spatial context of multiple landmarks rather than relying solely on isolated local visual cues. Inspired by this, we propose a Spatial Context Modeling (SCM) module, which traverses feature maps along four orthogonal directions and models interactions among the resulting directional representations at each spatial position. By aggregating complementary directional observations, SCM constructs richer scene layout representations. Since reliable spatial context depends on robust visual features, we introduce a High-Frequency Information Guidance (HFIG) module that extracts stable structural cues from the frequency domain. By emphasizing edges and contours that are less sensitive to appearance variations, HFIG encourages the model to learn more robust representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance on standard cross-view geo-localization benchmarks while exhibiting strong robustness under more challenging scenarios with unknown orientations and limited fields of view (FoVs).

</details>


### [87] [What We Talk About When We Talk About Emotion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13558)
*Xingzu Zhan,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究情感图像编辑，通过fMRI编码模型分析神经表征，提出ROI-ReFT方法，在400个编辑请求子集上达到91.75%目标情感准确率与83.25%联合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感图像编辑需在保留核心内容的同时改变场景传达的情感。由于同一场景可承载不同情感解读，实现情感目标需要基于源图像上下文的视觉改动。受Component Process Model（将情感反应与事件评价相联系）启发，作者尝试用神经表征作为这类编辑决策的参考依据。

Method: 使用TRIBE v2（fMRI编码模型）分析八个情感类别下的400个源-目标图像对。发现EmoEdit、EmoEditor和AffectDelta的隐藏状态与预测目标图像反应的对应关系在视觉区域强于情感相关区域，这一不平衡促使提出ROI-ReFT。ROI-ReFT将源图像的视觉反应作为内容参考，将预测的情感反应变化作为编辑方向，通过两个相互作用的支路将两种条件注入冻结的编辑器以引导生成过程；并用生成图像的预测反应来监督情感变化、约束视觉漂移。

Result: 在事后筛选的400个编辑请求子集上，ROI-ReFT取得91.75%的目标情感准确率和83.25%的联合成功率（即输出同时匹配目标情感且源-输出DINO-I至少为0.80）。

Conclusion: 基于fMRI编码模型的神经表征可有效指导情感图像编辑决策，ROI-ReFT通过结合内容参考与情感编辑方向，在保持内容的同时实现高精度的情感转换。代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Emotional image editing aims to change the emotion a scene conveys while preserving its essential content. Because the same scene can support different emotional interpretations, achieving an emotional goal requires visual changes grounded in the source context. Motivated by the Component Process Model, which links emotional responses to event appraisal, we investigate neural representations as a reference for these editing decisions. Using TRIBE v2, an fMRI encoding model, we analyze 400 source-target pairs across eight emotion categories. Hidden states from EmoEdit, EmoEditor, and AffectDelta correspond more strongly to predicted target-image responses in visual regions than in affect-related regions. This imbalance motivates ROI-ReFT, which combines source visual responses as a content reference with a predicted affective response change as an editing direction. Two interacting branches use both conditions to guide a frozen editor throughout generation. Predicted responses of the generated image supervise affective change and constrain visual drift. On a post hoc selected subset of 400 editing requests, ROI-ReFT achieves 91.75% target-emotion accuracy and 83.25% joint success, with the same output both matching the target emotion and reaching source-output DINO-I of at least 0.80. Our code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [88] [Thyroid Nodule Segmentation in Ultrasound Images with Conformal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13604)
*Francesco Teodori,Pierpaolo Brutti,Umberto Ferraro Petrillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一个甲状腺结节分割框架，融合DeepLabV3+、通道注意力模块和后处理保形预测，在TN3K基准上实现更优分割并提供像素级不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺结节在人群中发病率高，虽多为良性但恶性比例不可忽略。超声是主要诊断手段，而准确分割是CAD系统进行恶性分类和转移风险评估的前提，因此需要高精度且可靠的分割方法。

Method: 将甲状腺结节超声分割作为流水线第一阶段（后续为恶性分类与转移风险预测）。采用DeepLabV3+，其ASPP捕获多尺度上下文，骨干为EfficientNet-B5以平衡精度与效率；测试三种通道注意力模块（SE、CBAM、GCA）并搭配两种损失（Dice-BCE与Boundary）；在训练模型外包裹分裂式保形预测层，生成具有统计覆盖保证的像素级不确定性图。

Result: 在TN3K公开基准上用固定划分评估所有配置，并与最先进方法直接比较。仅基础骨干已优于此前报道结果，其中CBAM + Dice-BCE为综合最佳配置。

Conclusion: 通道注意力（尤其CBAM）结合Dice-BCE可有效提升甲状腺结节分割性能，且保形预测层能在不明显损害精度的前提下提供具有覆盖保证的像素级不确定性，提升CAD系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Thyroid nodules affect a large proportion of the general population. Although most are benign, a non-negligible fraction is malignant. Ultrasound imaging is the primary diagnostic modality, and accurate nodule segmentation is a prerequisite for CAD systems to classify malignancy and assess metastatic risk.\\ In this work, we propose a thyroid nodule segmentation framework that integrates a DeepLabV3+, channel-attention modules and post-hoc conformal prediction, providing both accurate mask and pixel-level uncertainty estimates. We address thyroid ultrasound nodule segmentation as the first stage of a pipeline that also includes malignancy classification and metastatic risk prediction. We adopt DeepLabV3+, whose Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling (ASPP) captures multi-scale context, with an EfficientNet-B5 backbone for its accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Additionally, we test three channel-attention modules: Squeeze-and-Excitation, CBAM and Grouped Channel Attention (GCA), paired with two loss formulations (Dice-BCE and Boundary).\\ Since binary masks do not indicate prediction confidence, we wrap the trained model with a split conformal prediction layer that produces pixel-level uncertainty maps with statistical coverage guarantees.\\ We evaluate all configurations on the TN3K public benchmark using a fixed split and directly compare them with state-of-the-art methods. The plain backbone already improves upon previously reported results, with CBAM + Dice-BCE emerging as the best overall configuration.

</details>


### [89] [CUBE: Class-conditioned Unknown Boundary Evidence for Open-Set Insect Species Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13610)
*Hao Tan,Qimin Zhang,Zhe Xiao,Zekai Zuo,Wanting Liu,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CUBE类条件未知边界证据框架，将未知性建模于各已知物种周围，实现细粒度未知昆虫的局部拒绝，并扩展CUBE-E引入地理一致性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集昆虫识别中，细粒度未知物种通常视觉上不异常，而是靠近某些已知物种的决策边界，容易导致闭集预测不可靠，传统全局未知建模难以应对。

Method: CUBE基于证据学习，从辅助物种中学习类条件边界证据，推理时保留辅助物种作为影子参考，并加入分类学候选校准以验证生物学合理性；CUBE-E在可用生态元数据时增加轻量位置一致性项，不修改视觉主干。

Result: 在Open-Insect C-America基准上，CUBE在local、non-local、non-moth未知划分上AUROC分别为95.8%、98.7%、99.1%，闭集准确率94.9%；CUBE-E提升至96.0%、99.7%、99.5%，闭集准确率95.2%。

Conclusion: 类条件、局部化的未知边界建模优于全局未知类方案，结合分类学校准与生态元数据可进一步提升开放集细粒度昆虫识别性能。

Abstract: Open-set insect species recognition faces significant challenges when deployed in real-world biodiversity scenarios, particularly when encountering unknown species that are visually close to known training classes. Unlike generic out-of-distribution samples, fine-grained unknown insects are often not visually anomalous. They instead tend to appear near the decision boundary of specific known species, leading to unreliable closed-set predictions. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{Class-conditioned Unknown Boundary Evidence (CUBE)}, a novel evidence-based framework that models unknownness around each known species rather than as a single global unknown class. Our key idea is to evaluate a test sample relative to the known species it most closely resembles, enabling local rejection for visually confusable unknown insects. CUBE learns class-conditioned boundary evidence from auxiliary species, preserves auxiliary species as shadow references during inference, and incorporates taxonomic candidate calibration to verify biological plausibility. When ecological metadata is available, we further introduce CUBE-E, which adds a lightweight location-consistency term without modifying the visual backbone. Experiments on the Open-Insect C-America benchmark show that CUBE achieves superior performance, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines with 95.8\%, 98.7\%, and 99.1\% AUROC on local, non-local, and non-moth unknown splits, respectively, while maintaining 94.9\% closed-set accuracy. With ecological metadata, CUBE-E further improves the results to 96.0\%, 99.7\%, and 99.5\% AUROC with 95.2\% closed-set accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [Benchmarking Answer-Grounding Consistency in Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13636)
*Ping Guo,Zhiqi Huang,Xinran Li,Linkun Yu,Jingyin Pei,Kamata Seiichiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出AESP协议评估遥感视觉问答中答案与定位证据的一致性，并构建G-RSVQA基准，发现现有模型存在严重的证据支持失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有grounded遥感VQA评估仅衡量答案正确性与定位质量，未直接检验答案与定位证据之间的一致性，导致可能忽略模型"答对但依据错误"的失效模式。

Method: 提出Answer-Evidence Support Protocol（AESP），基于证据支持度评估一致性，即定位证据是否支持模型自身答案；针对存在性、计数与比较三类问题设计任务特定规则；并构建G-RSVQA基准，含15,027个样本，提供问题、参考答案及相关对象的完整标注。

Result: 在七个grounded视觉-语言模型上实验，三类问题均出现证据支持失效；在证据评估子集中，高达50.82%的正确答案未被所提供的定位证据支持。

Conclusion: 答案正确性与定位质量不足以反映模型推理的一致性，AESP与G-RSVQA揭示了grounded RSVQA中普遍存在的答案-证据不一致问题，为更严格的评估提供了协议与基准。

Abstract: In grounded remote sensing visual question answering, a model produces a textual answer and identifies relevant objects in the image as grounding evidence. Existing evaluations measure answer correctness and localization quality, but do not directly assess answer-grounding consistency. We propose the Answer-Evidence Support Protocol (AESP) to assess this consistency through evidence support: whether the grounding evidence supports the model's own answer. AESP applies task-specific rules to existence, counting, and comparison questions. We further construct the Grounded Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering (G-RSVQA) benchmark, containing 15,027 samples with questions, reference answers, and all related object-level annotations. Experiments on seven grounded vision--language models reveal failures of evidence support across all three question types. Up to 50.82% of correct answers in the evidence evaluation subset are not supported by the provided grounding evidence.

</details>


### [91] [PhysForge: Material-Controllable 3D Reconstruction with Separable Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13681)
*Yuchen Wang,Yanqi Bao,Zheng Gu,Zhengqing Chen,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForge是一个基于视频扩散模型(VDM)的物理3D生成框架，包含PhysFit与PhysComp两个模块，实现材料可控的物理拟合与组件级交互建模，在材料依赖的物理响应控制上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理3D生成方法对材质行为的控制有限，主要依赖外观推断，且难以从物体与语义部件间的复杂交互中估计物理属性。

Method: 提出PhysForge框架，含两个互补模块：PhysFit利用材质条件化的VDM动力学作为物理属性拟合的参考，引入窗口化拟合(windowed fitting)聚焦于揭示材质的交互，并用Hallucinated Forcing将VDM幻觉出的非物理运动与真实材质响应解耦；PhysComp将复杂交互分解为单个物体与语义组件，使其物理参数可独立优化并灵活组合以进行联合仿真。

Result: 在PhysAssets和Google Scanned Objects数据集上，PhysForge在常见与罕见材质上均取得最佳CLIP与物理可控性分数，质性对比、组件级消融与用户研究进一步支持其改进。

Conclusion: PhysForge相比现有方法提供了更强的材料依赖物理响应控制能力，并支持复杂交互的组件级物理建模。

Abstract: Recent advances in physical 3D generation have enabled visually faithful assets with simulatable mechanical behavior. However, existing methods provide limited control over material behavior beyond appearance-based inference and struggle to estimate physical properties from complex interactions between objects and semantic parts. Therefore, we present PhysForge, a Video Diffusion Model (VDM)-based framework for addressing these challenges, comprising two complementary modules for material-controllable physical fitting and componentlevel interaction modeling. The first, PhysFit, leverages material-conditioned VDM dynamics as references for physical-property fitting, introducing windowed fitting to focus on material-revealing interactions and Hallucinated Forcing to disentangle nonphysical motion hallucinated by the VDM from material responses. The second, PhysComp decomposes complex interactions into individual objects and semantic components, enabling their physical parameters to be optimized independently and flexibly composed for joint simulation. Experiments on PhysAssets and Google Scanned Objects demonstrate that, compared with existing methods, PhysForge provides stronger control over material-dependent physical responses while supporting component-level physical modeling for complex interactions. It achieves the best CLIP and physical controllability scores on both common and uncommon materials, with qualitative comparisons, component-level ablations, and a user study further supporting these improvements. Our project page is available at https://masterwanna.github.io/PhysForge.html .

</details>


### [92] [X-AVDD: Cross-Attentive Audio-Visual Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13687)
*Saumyaranjan Mohanty,Aravind Reddy,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出X-AVDD，一种基于交叉注意力的音视频数据集蒸馏方法，通过解耦跨模态对齐与联合优化，显著降低内存开销并提升跨模态语义保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频数据集蒸馏方法（如DM、AVDD）存在两大局限：一是内存密集，难以扩展到复杂架构和高SPC设置；二是难以保持强跨模态语义对应关系。

Method: X-AVDD采用交叉注意力机制，在合成过程中显式捕捉模态间关系；通过将跨模态对齐从联合优化中解耦，实现更高的内存效率。

Result: 端到端合成成本降低约8倍GPU小时；在VGGSound上SPC=10时top-1准确率从8.2%提升至24.0%，SPC=20时从9.8%提升至26.5%；在AVE上SPC=50对ViT目标的跨架构泛化从25.5%提升至35.3%；在AudioCLIP上仅用5.65%数据达62.3%准确率（全数据集为72.4%），训练时间减少9倍。

Conclusion: X-AVDD显著优于现有音视频蒸馏方法，兼具内存高效、跨模态对齐良好、可扩展至复杂架构等优势，验证了解耦跨模态对齐策略的有效性。

Abstract: Audio-visual dataset distillation aims to synthesize a small dataset from a large paired audio-visual dataset, while nearly preserving the training utility for neural networks. Existing approaches such as DM and AVDD face two critical limitations: 1) they are memory-intensive, making them difficult to scale to complex architectures and higher samples-per-class (SPC) settings, and 2) they often fail to preserve strong cross-modal semantic correspondence. To tackle these issues, we propose X-AVDD, a cross-attention-based distillation method that explicitly captures inter-modal relationships during synthesis. By decoupling cross-modal alignment from joint optimization, X-AVDD is substantially more memory-efficient, reducing end-to-end synthesis cost by $\sim 8\times$ GPU-hours, while producing highly aligned synthetic data. Empirically, X-AVDD significantly outperforms other audio-visual distillation methods. On VGGSound, an audio-visual dataset of short YouTube clips, X-AVDD improves top-1 test accuracy from $8.2\%\rightarrow24.0\%$ at SPC=$10$ and $9.8\%\rightarrow26.5\%$ at SPC=$20$. Furthermore, X-AVDD improves cross-architecture generalization (e.g., $25.5\%\rightarrow35.3\%$ for a ViT target on AVE dataset at SPC $=50$) and successfully scales to complex architectures such as AudioCLIP, achieving $62.3\%$ accuracy using only $5.65\%$ of the full dataset, against the full dataset accuracy of $72.4\%$ while completing in $9\times$ lower training time compared to the full dataset.

</details>


### [93] [Improving Semantic Coverage in AI Test & Evaluation through Generative Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13697)
*Connor Hashemi,Bharadwaj Ravichandran,Connor Anderson,Shitij Govil,Brandon RichardWebster,Brian Hu,Keith Fieldhouse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段生成式AI流水线，在无真值语义标签条件下检测并填补测试集的语义覆盖缺口（如昼夜、天气），仅用于扩充测试集而无需重训练模型，从而提升AI模型测试与评估结论的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI模型的测试与评估(T&E)中，评估者通常仅能访问一个留出的测试数据集，对训练数据及其语义构成一无所知。若关键输入条件（如时段、天气、场景位置）在测试集中代表性不足，会产生语义覆盖缺口，导致T&E结果产生误导。因此需要在无这些语义真值标签的情况下检测并填补此类缺口。

Method: 提出三阶段生成式AI流水线：(1) 用视觉-语言模型(VLM)对语义因子做零样本二分类标注，并通过嵌入空间聚类识别对下游模型行为影响最大的语义；(2) 用基于扩散的图像到图像模型，将已有被覆盖图像转换到欠代表语义域，在保留场景内容的同时增强高层外观；(3) 对增强图像嵌入进行统计分析，验证合成图像在纳入前确实提升了数据集覆盖度。关键点：合成数据仅用于丰富测试集，T&E过程不做任何模型重训练。

Result: 在图像分类和目标检测两类任务上、以昼夜语义缺口为验证场景，证明了该流水线有效：增强后的测试数据集能更好地分辨"接触过"与"未接触过"缺失语义的模型，从而使T&E结论更接近理想全语义覆盖数据集所得出的结论。

Conclusion: 该三阶段生成式流水线可在无语义真值标签的情况下检测并填补测试集的语义覆盖缺口，且仅扩充测试集、不重训练模型，即可显著改善T&E结论与理想全覆盖数据的一致性。代码已开源。

Abstract: In test and evaluation (T&E) of AI models, evaluators typically have access only to a held-out test dataset and no knowledge of the training data or its semantic composition. If critical input conditions---such as time-of-day, weather, or scene location---are underrepresented in this test dataset, T&E results can be misleadingly due to this semantic coverage gap. We propose a three-stage generative AI pipeline to detect and fill such semantic gaps without requiring ground-truth labels of these semantics. First, vision-language models (VLMs) perform zero-shot binary labeling of semantic factors, and an embedding-space clustering step identifies which semantics most affect downstream model behavior. Second, diffusion-based image-to-image models transform existing covered images into the underrepresented semantic domain, preserving scene content while augmenting high-level appearance. Third, statistical analysis of the augmented image embeddings verifies that the synthetic imagery improves dataset coverage before it is incorporated. Crucially, the synthetic data is used only to enrich the test set and no model retraining is performed for T&E. We validate the full pipeline on both image classification and object detection under day/night semantic gaps, showing that the augmented test datasets better distinguish models with and without exposure to the missing semantic, thereby yielding T&E conclusions that more closely match those of an ideal, fully covered dataset. Code available at https://github.com/Hashe037/DEDUCE

</details>


### [94] [GLIMPSE: Global-Local Indexing with Multi-Precision Spectral Encoding for Explorable Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13706)
*Zhufeng Xu,Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Yikang Ding,Xiaoqiang Liu,Guoxin Zhang,Hongbo Fu,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLIMPSE：带多精度坐标与几何条件谱衰减的全局-局部记忆接口，用于相机控制视频世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机控制视频世界模型中，当相机回到先前位置时需保持场景一致；现有将特征渲染到目标帧的记忆方法会把深度与位姿误差转化为重影或重复结构。

Method: 提出GLIMPSE：全局-局部记忆接口，采用band-wise多精度坐标与几何条件谱衰减降低对不准确投影地址的敏感度；局部patch记忆提供视角特定外观，循环全局记忆提供场景上下文；以固定局部读取预算进行footprint引导检索。

Result: 在匹配检索与读取预算下，GLIMPSE在几何对应与光度一致性上优于确定性位置迁移。

Conclusion: 多精度坐标与几何条件谱衰减能提升相机回访一致性，优于位置迁移基线。

Abstract: A camera-controlled video world model must keep a scene consistent when the camera returns to it. Memories that render stored features into the target frame can turn depth and pose errors into ghosting or duplicated structure. Positional transport avoids that resampling by relocating a historical patch's address rather than its content, but assigns full positional precision even to inaccurate projections. We present GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface with band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses. Local patch memory supplies view-specific appearance, while recurrent global memory provides scene context. Footprint-guided retrieval selects historical patches under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.

</details>


### [95] [State-Isolated Vision Mamba via Update-Tendency Routing for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13720)
*Yan Luo,Laibin Chang,Zefan Feng,Yi'ang Chen,Kui Jiang,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出状态争用（State Contention）问题，并基于此提出状态隔离（State Isolation）原则与 IsoMamba 架构，通过 Δ 引导的聚类-路由-隔离范式实现水下图像增强的 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision Mamba 研究主要聚焦于 token 序列化、扫描策略或专家路由，却忽视了一个状态层面问题：对于空间异质的视觉复原任务，循环状态应如何组织。作者指出，强制更新行为异质的 token 共同循环修改共享隐状态会引发持续的跨内容干扰，即状态争用。

Method: 提出状态隔离原则：更新倾向兼容的 token 在独立循环状态流中演化，按 token 兼容性而非 token 排序组织状态演化。实例化为 IsoMamba 架构：Δ 引导构造模型内在更新表示，通过无标签聚类发现更新兼容的 token 分组，并遵循聚类-路由-隔离范式将其路由至隔离的 Mamba 专家。此外提出动态通道重加权目标以补偿水下图像随波长变化的复原难度。

Result: 在多个水下图像增强基准上持续取得 SOTA 性能，同时提供更清晰的隐状态响应，验证了状态隔离循环建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 按 token 兼容性组织循环状态演化优于按 token 排序，状态隔离是缓解状态争用、提升 Vision Mamba 空间异质视觉复原能力的有效建模原则。

Abstract: Vision Mamba has recently emerged as an efficient alternative to convolutional and Transformer-based architectures by modeling long-range spatial dependencies through recurrent state evolution. Existing studies primarily improve token serialization, scanning strategies, or expert routing, yet largely overlook a state level question question: how should recurrent states be organized for spatially heterogeneous visual restoration? In this paper, we show that forcing tokens with heterogeneous update behaviors to recurrently modify a shared hidden state leads to persistent cross-content interference, which we term \textbf{State Contention}. We further argue that effective recurrent modeling should organize state evolution according to token compatibility rather than token ordering. Based on this observation, we introduce \textbf{State Isolation}, a new recurrent state organization principle for Vision Mamba,tokens with compatible state-update tendencies evolve within independent recurrent state streams. To instantiate this principle, we propose \textbf{IsoMamba}, a $Δ$-guided architecture that constructs model-intrinsic update representations, discovers update-compatible token groups through label-free clustering, and routes them to isolated Mamba experts following a \textbf{cluster--route--isolate} paradigm. In addition, we develop a dynamic channel-wise reweighting objective to compensate for wavelength-dependent reconstruction difficulty in underwater images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IsoMamba consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks while providing cleaner hidden state responses, validating the effectiveness of state isolated recurrent modeling.

</details>


### [96] [LAPT-Scale: Scaling Embodied Policies with Latent Action Pre-Training on Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13732)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Peidong Liu,Mingyang Li,Jiawei Li,Wenhow Li,Wenbo Ding,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAPT-Scale 提出可扩展的潜在动作预训练范式，用连续潜在动作从大规模第一视角人类视频中提取监督信号，预训练 VLA 策略，将真实机器人任务成功率从 63.3% 提升至 85.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略的扩展受限于具身交互数据采集成本高；大规模第一视角人类视频是丰富替代来源，但缺乏机器人动作标签，现有动作标注方法在真实视觉复杂性与多样化操作动力学下噪声较大。

Method: 包含两部分：LAPT-Scale-LAM 通过解释人类视频中帧间视觉转移学习连续潜在动作，作为人类-derived 监督；LAPT-Scale-Policy 通过人类视频潜在动作预训练、目标机器人具身对齐与任务特定后训练来训练 VLA 策略。与以往向量量化 LAM 不同，采用连续潜在空间保留细粒度运动与交互动力学，并融合感知、语义与光流对齐信号改进转移级监督。

Result: 在 23.3K 小时第一视角视频上训练，LAPT-Scale-LAM 为策略预训练提供隐式动作监督，随标注人类视频规模从 1K 增至 5K 小时，取得一致的扩展增益，真实机器人成功率由 63.3% 提升至 85.0%，且学得策略可泛化到未见视觉条件。

Conclusion: 连续潜在动作预训练可有效利用大规模人类视频实现机器人策略的可扩展学习，显著提升真实机器人表现并具备视觉泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. We introduce LAPT-Scale, a scalable latent action pre-training paradigm with two components: LAPT-Scale-LAM learns continuous latent actions by explaining frame-to-frame visual transitions in human videos and uses them as human-derived supervision, while LAPT-Scale-Policy trains a VLA policy through human-video latent-action pre-training, target-robot embodiment alignment, and task-specific post-training. Unlike prior vector-quantized LAMs, LAPT-Scale-LAM uses a continuous latent space to preserve fine-grained motion and interaction dynamics. We further combine perceptual, semantic, and optical-flow alignment signals to improve transition-level supervision beyond pixel reconstruction. Trained on 23.3K hours of egocentric video, LAPT-Scale-LAM provides implicit action supervision for policy pre-training, yielding consistent scaling gains and improving real-robot success from 63.3% to 85.0% as annotated human video scales from 1K to 5K hours. The learned policy also generalizes to unseen visual conditions.

</details>


### [97] [KVeil: First-Place Solution to ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13733)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告提出ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge两阶段的冠军方案，通过不可察觉扰动阻止人脸视频被未授权定制化生成，单扰动同时防御参考式与微调式定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视频生成使少量人脸视频即可复现身份，带来严重身份隐私风险；需在有限且不可察觉的扰动预算下防止人脸视频被未授权定制。

Method: Phase 1提出Reference Capacity Collapse（一对互补损失，在保持后续视频表示依赖参考条件的同时降低其信息量）与Motion-Guided EOT（提升对未知推理条件的鲁棒性）；Phase 2提出两层框架，对人脸与背景区域分别保护，前者基于合成目标身份构造结构对齐扰动，后者强化Phase 1的白盒破坏。均基于LETHE工作。

Result: 在白盒参考式定制下实现近乎完美的保护，并在其他定制范式、多种模型及时间攻击下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 单一扰动可同时抵御参考式与基于微调的定制及未见生成模型，期望推动人脸视频防未授权定制研究，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid development of personalized video generation has raised serious concerns over identity privacy, as a few facial videos of a person now suffice to reproduce their identity in synthetic videos. This report presents the first-place solutions to both phases of the ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos, which aims to protect facial videos from unauthorized customization under a limited and imperceptible perturbation budget. For Phase 1, we introduce Reference Capacity Collapse, a pair of complementary losses that degrade the information carried by the reference condition while keeping subsequent video representations dependent on it, together with a Motion-Guided EOT mechanism that improves robustness across unknown inference conditions. Phase 2 further considers tuning-based customization and unseen generative models. To address both threats within a single perturbation, we design a two-layer framework that protects the facial and background regions separately, constructing structure-aligned perturbations from a synthetic target identity in the former and strengthening the white-box disruption of Phase 1 in the latter. Our method achieves nearly perfect protection under white-box reference-based customization, and remains effective under other customization paradigms, various models, and temporal attacks. Both solutions are built upon our recent work LETHE. We hope our solutions can inspire future work on protecting facial videos against unauthorized customization. Our code is available at https://github.com/AntigoneRandy/KVeil.

</details>


### [98] [Gaussian Splatting with Reflections Guided by What is Seen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13757)
*Yiming Liang,Tianyu Xiao,Hiroshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于2D Gaussian Splatting的框架，直接利用输入图像中可观测的环境信息，结合混合反射外观表示与视角相关镜面建模，在近场反射场景中实现更优的渲染质量和几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF和3DGS在普通场景表现优异，但难以捕捉视角相关镜面效果，尤其是近场反射。现有方法使用环境贴图（仅表示无穷远光照）或环境高斯（仅依赖反射的间接监督），不足以准确建模此类效果。

Method: 基于2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) 构建框架，直接利用输入图像中可观测的环境信息，并集成混合反射外观表示与视角相关镜面建模。

Result: 在公开基准以及新构建的近场反射数据集上，方法取得了更优的渲染质量和几何精度。

Conclusion: 直接利用输入图像中可观测的环境信息并结合混合反射表示与视角相关镜面建模，可有效提升近场反射场景的新视角合成质量与几何精度。

Abstract: While novel view synthesis methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have shown impressive performance on common scenes, they often struggle with capturing view-dependent specular effects, particularly near-field reflections. Existing methods utilize environment maps, which only represent lights from infinity, or environment Gaussians, which rely solely on indirect supervision from reflections, are insufficient for accurately modeling such effects. Here, we propose a framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) that leverages directly observable environment information in input images. Our approach also integrates a hybrid reflection appearance representation and view-dependent specular modeling. Experiments on public benchmarks and a newly curated near-field reflection dataset demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [Neurons++: A Unified Visual-Stream Framework for Image and Video Reconstruction from fMRI with Neural-to-Visual Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13763)
*Haonan Wang,Yuchen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Qixiang Zhang,Lehan Wang,Xuanqi Huang,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neurons++，一个面向静态图像与动态视频的视觉流 fMRI 重建框架，采用皮层启发的串行解码范式，在标准基准上实现 SOTA 视频重建与有竞争力的图像重建，并展现出神经扰动下的可控性与 ROI 引导的语义调制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 fMRI 解码视觉体验对理解人类感知与开发脑机接口具有重要意义。现有生成式解码方法虽能重建看似合理的图像与视频，但视觉合理性并不等于输出忠实于脑活动，因此需要一种更忠实、更受大脑信号约束的重建框架。

Method: 提出 Neurons++，将此前只做视频重建的 Neurons 扩展为图像与视频统一的视觉流重建。方法采用串行、受皮层机制启发的解码流程，替代原有的并行任务条件化：先通过图像-fMRI 预训练获得稳定的空间-语义初始化，再分别针对图像和视频任务进行被试特异性微调；串行设计将 fMRI 表征组织为显式的空间、语义、语言层级与时间线索。

Result: 大量实验表明，Neurons++ 在标准基准上取得 SOTA 视频重建效果并具备有竞争力的图像重建性能。此外，神经-视觉分析显示：模型在神经扰动下具有定性的方向可控性，其 fMRI 编码器读出对刺激相关的皮层特征敏感，并支持 ROI 引导的语义调制。

Conclusion: Neurons++ 通过串行皮层启发式解码与分阶段预训练-微调策略，提升了 fMRI 到视觉流的忠实重建能力，并在可控性与皮层特征敏感性上提供了解释性证据，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from fMRI is important for understanding human perception and developing brain-computer interfaces. Recent generative decoding methods can reconstruct plausible images and videos, but visual plausibility alone does not ensure that the output is faithfully grounded in brain activity. In this work, we propose Neurons++, a visual-stream fMRI reconstruction framework for both static image and dynamic video reconstruction. Compared with our conference version Neurons, Neurons++ extends the paradigm from video-only reconstruction to visual-stream reconstruction for both images and videos, and replaces parallel task conditioning with serial cortex-inspired decoding. The model first uses image-fMRI pretraining to obtain a stable spatial-semantic initialization, and then performs subject-specific fine-tuning for image and video reconstruction separately. The serial design organizes fMRI-derived representations into explicit spatial, semantic, language-level, and temporal cues. Extensive experiments show that Neurons++ achieves state-of-the-art video reconstruction and competitive image reconstruction across standard benchmarks. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, neural-to-visual analyses indicate that Neurons++ exhibits qualitative directional controllability under neural perturbation, that its fMRI encoder read-out is sensitive to stimulus-relevant cortical features, and that it supports ROI-guided semantic modulation. Code and model weights are available at: https://github.com/xmed-lab/NeuronsPlusPlus.

</details>


### [100] [Shortcut Learning in Geospatial Foundation Model Embeddings for Cross-Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13766)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究地理空间基础模型在灾害损害评估中的"捷径学习"问题：按整场灾害留出评估时性能大幅下降，说明模型依赖事件/场景线索而非可迁移证据；并提出仅用训练灾害学习的特征变换可提升对未见灾害的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型可用于灾害损害评估，但在熟悉灾害上的高精度可能掩盖捷径学习——即基于事件或场景特征进行预测，而非依赖能迁移到新灾害的证据。因此需要按未见灾害域进行评估，揭示模型鲁棒性真实水平。

Method: 研究TerraMind、Prithvi-EO-2.0、DOFA和DINOv3的嵌入表示，在多灾害数据集xView2和BRIGHT上开展实验。评估设置对比整场灾害留出与随机划分图像块。通过四组测试考察预测依据的信息：事件与采集上下文、灾前/灾后图像输入、建筑数量、正确图像配对。在此基础上，测试仅由训练灾害学习得到的特征变换（监督对比学习、PCA等）对未见灾害泛化的影响。

Result: 整场灾害留出（而非随机划分图像块）使xView2平均平衡准确率由0.807降至0.638，BRIGHT由0.818降至0.568。灾前图像仍具预测性；仅输入标注建筑数量的分类器在xView2和BRIGHT上分别达0.629和0.653。灾前+灾后特征组合在xView2平均表现最佳，但在BRIGHT未优于仅灾前特征。同一灾害、同一损害类别内打乱图像配对仅造成轻微性能损失。特征变换可提升性能，其中监督对比学习在xView2最佳，PCA在BRIGHT最佳。

Conclusion: 在未见域上评估对衡量模型鲁棒性至关重要；简单的特征级变换可提升对未见灾害的泛化能力。代码与实验见 https://github.com/alishibli97/gfm-shortcut-learning。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can support disaster damage assessment, but high accuracy on familiar disasters may hide shortcut learning: prediction from event or scene characteristics rather than evidence that transfers to a new disaster. We study embeddings from TerraMind, Prithvi-EO-2.0, DOFA, and DINOv3 on the multi-disaster datasets xView2 and BRIGHT. Holding out an entire disaster, instead of randomly splitting image tiles, reduces mean balanced accuracy from 0.807 to 0.638 on xView2 and from 0.818 to 0.568 on BRIGHT. Four sets of tests examine what information supports prediction: event and acquisition context, before/after image inputs, building count, and correct image pairing. Pre-event images remain predictive, and a classifier given only the annotated number of buildings reaches balanced accuracy of 0.629 and 0.653. Combining before/after features performs best on average on xView2, but does not improve over pre-only features on BRIGHT. Shuffling image pairs within the same disaster and damage class causes modest losses. Motivated by these findings, we test whether feature transformations learned only from training disasters improve generalization to unseen disasters. These transformations improve performance, with supervised contrastive learning performing best on xView2 and PCA on BRIGHT. Our results highlight the importance of evaluation on unseen domains for assessing model robustness and show that simple feature-level transformations can improve generalization. Code and experiments can be found at https://github.com/alishibli97/gfm-shortcut-learning.

</details>


### [101] [READ: Reading the Selective Scan for Faithful Video Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13776)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Piotr Kubaty,Arkadiusz Lewicki,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 READ，一种针对选择性状态空间模型（SSM）的无参数归因方法，通过展开分类器读取位置的递归，将循环状态中的贡献回溯到视频 token，仅需一次前向传播即可生成时空相关性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型高效适用于长序列视频建模，但其递归计算难以确定哪些视频 token 驱动了预测。现有归因方法通常依赖额外的反向传播或重复模型评估，计算开销远高于其所解释的前向推理。

Method: READ 将递归在分类器读取（readout）位置进行展开，把累积在循环状态中的贡献回溯至参与最终预测的视频 token。归因锚定于分类器读取点，从而生成单一时空相关性图，可据此直接获取帧级与区域级相关性。方法为无参数，且仅需一次前向传播。

Result: 尽管仅需一次前向传播，READ 在忠实度与定位能力上可与开销高得多的归因方法相竞争。实验表明 READ 避免了现有方法的系统性相关性偏差，并在不同数据集与骨干网络上恢复出有意义的时序结构与类别特定结构。

Conclusion: 结果表明选择性状态空间模型的递归不仅是高效的推理机制，也是其预测的可解释证据的直接来源。

Abstract: Selective state-space models are an efficient approach to long-sequence video modeling. However, their recurrent computation makes it difficult to determine which video tokens drive a prediction. Existing attribution methods often require additional backward passes or repeated model evaluations, making them substantially more expensive than the forward inference they aim to explain. In this work, we introduce READ, a parameter-free attribution method for selective state-space models that unrolls the recurrence at the position read by the classifier. READ traces the contribution accumulated in the recurrent state back to the video tokens that contribute to the final prediction.
