By anchoring attribution at the classifier's readout, it produces a single spatiotemporal relevance map from which frame- and region-level relevance can be obtained directly. Despite requiring only one forward pass, READ achieves competitive faithfulness and localisation compared with substantially more expensive attribution methods. Experimental results show that READ avoids the systematic relevance biases of existing methods while recovering meaningful temporal and class-specific structure across datasets and backbones. More broadly, our results show that the recurrence underlying selective state-space models is not only an efficient mechanism for inference, but also a direct source of interpretable evidence for their predictions.

</details>


### [102] [Detection of extremely rare dead cells in fluorescence microscopy images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13780)
*Prateek Verma,Benjamin Butter,Jian Zhang,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了启发式方法、分类方法与异常检测方法在荧光显微图像凋亡细胞检测中的表现，基于258张图像、65,527个细胞核、243个标注死细胞的数据集，发现基于图像特征的深度学习分类（ResNet-18）与基于数值特征的XGBoost表现最佳，显著优于手工启发式和异常检测方法，但整体任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微图像中死细胞检测对表型筛选和药物疗效评估至关重要，但自动化识别面临信号变异性、光学伪影、多通道共定位歧义等问题：定义核边界的标记与凋亡标记在空间和时间上并不完全一致，且标记积累动力学在不同细胞和凋亡阶段间存在差异，导致通用阈值不可靠。因此需要系统比较不同计算方法以寻找有效解决方案。

Method: 在核染标本上系统比较三类方法：(1) 启发式方法，使用强度阈值、分布、相关性和领域引导；(2) 分类方法，包括数值特征（Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM）和图像特征（ResNet-18、EfficientNet-B0）；(3) 异常检测，包括数值特征（Isolation Forest、one-class SVM、PCA重构）和图像特征（卷积自编码器、Deep SVDD）。数据集包含258张图像、65,527个细胞/细胞核、243个人工标注的真值死细胞，评价指标为PR-AUC、Precision、Recall、F1-score和macro-F1。

Result: 基于图像特征的分类（深度学习）和基于数值特征的分类（传统方法）取得最佳性能，其中ResNet-18和XGBoost显著优于人工设计的启发式方法和异常检测方法。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，即使采用当前最先进的计算与学习技术，此类生物医学问题仍极具挑战性。研究目标是为研究人员提供有效的决策支持工具，既可用于全自动处理，也可用于人机协同（human-in-the-loop）辅助领域科学家。

Abstract: Dead cell detection in fluorescence microscopy is essential for phenotypic screening and drug efficacy assessment. However, automated identification remains challenging due to signal variability, optical artifacts, and colocalization ambiguities across multiple channels. Markers that define nuclear boundaries do not neatly coincide spatially or temporally with apoptotic markers. Furthermore, marker accumulation kinetics vary across individual cells and apoptosis stages, making universal thresholds unreliable. We present a systematic comparison of several computational approaches for detecting apoptotic cells in nucleus-stained specimens: (1) heuristic methods using intensity thresholds, distributions, correlations, and domain guidance; (2) classification using numerical features (Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost and LightGBM) and image features (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0); and (3) anomaly detection, also using numerical features (Isolation Forest, one-class SVM and PCA reconstruction) and image features (convolutional autoencoders and Deep SVDD). Using a dataset of 258 images containing 65,527 cells/nuclei across multiple scenes with 243 manually annotated ground truth dead cells, we evaluate each method on metrics such as PR-AUC, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and macro-F1. Our results indicate that classification using image features (deep learning) and numerical features (traditional) achieved the best performance, with ResNet-18 and XGBoost significantly outperforming manually designed heuristics and anomaly detection methods. Our findings demonstrate that such biomedical problems remain highly challenging to solve even with the state-of-the-art computational and learning techniques. We aim to provide an effective decision-support tool to researchers while tackling such problems either fully-automatically or in a human-in-the-loop setting as an aide to domain scientists.

</details>


### [103] [SimVTA: Simulation-Grounded Video-Trajectory Alignment for Driving World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13781)
*Changhui Jing,Haochen Tian,Hongchen Li,Tianyu Li,Caojun Wang,Zihao Zhang,Yanhao Liu,Chengen Xie,Bailin Li,Hao Zhao,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimVTA：一种基于仿真的驾驶世界动作模型后训练框架，结合轨迹优化与视频监督，在NAVSIM与HUGSIM上显著提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界动作模型（WAMs）通过成对的轨迹与未来观测学习，并可用强化学习探索替代轨迹、借助驾驶奖励改进规划。但驾驶日志仅对已记录轨迹提供未来视频，替代轨迹缺乏对应的视觉监督，导致强化学习中的探索轨迹无法获得视觉指导。

Method: SimVTA首先对每个观测采样候选轨迹，利用真实世界模拟器（如World Engine）在经3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建的真实场景中，为高奖励候选渲染未来视频，从而获得独立于模型自身预测、与轨迹匹配的监督信号。在有限渲染预算下，优先为渲染候选中误差较大的未来提供视频监督。最后用GRPO优化轨迹规划，并在匹配视频上以流匹配损失更新共享表征。推理时模型直接预测轨迹，无需调用模拟器。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上，将SimVTA应用于WAM初始化使PDMS从86.2提升至91.3（+5.1）；在NAVSIM v2上达到88.2 EPDMS；在HUGSIM闭环评测中平均HD-Score为38.8。消融实验表明，用视频监督更新共享表征可改进规划，而在高奖励轨迹中优先监督高误差未来可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 以仿真渲染为替代轨迹提供轨迹匹配的视频监督，并与GRPO轨迹优化结合，能有效提升驾驶世界动作模型的规划能力，且推理阶段无需模拟器，兼顾性能与部署效率。

Abstract: Driving world action models (WAMs) learn from paired trajectories and future observations. Post-training with reinforcement learning allows the policy to explore alternative trajectories and improve planning using driving rewards. However, driving logs contain future videos only for the recorded trajectories, leaving these alternatives without corresponding visual supervision. Thus, we introduce SimVTA, a simulation-grounded post-training framework that combines trajectory optimization with video supervision. First, we sample candidate trajectories for each observation and use a real-world simulator (e.g., World Engine) to render future videos for high-reward candidates in real-world scenes reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). This provides trajectory-matched supervision independent of the model's own predictions. Under a limited rendering budget, we prioritize video supervision for higher-error futures among these rendered candidates. Finally, we optimize trajectory planning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and use a flow-matching loss on the matched videos to update shared representations. At inference time, the model predicts trajectories directly, without simulator calls. On NAVSIM v1, applying SimVTA to a WAM initialization improves PDMS from 86.2 to 91.3 (+5.1). It achieves 88.2 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and an average HD-Score of 38.8 in closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show that updating shared representations with video supervision improves planning, while prioritizing higher-error futures among high-reward trajectories further improves performance.

</details>


### [104] [SpatialASM: Internalizing Active Mentalization for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13832)
*Yuhong Liu,Shenxi Wu,Junyi Chi,Beichen Zhang,Xuanlang Dai,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active Spatial Mentalization (ASM) 概念与 SpatialASM 框架，通过训练时的空间模拟将主动视点规划与心理场景想象内化到 LVLM 中，在七个空间推理基准上对四种骨干模型均取得平均准确率提升，且保持原有通用视觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在解决复杂空间推理任务时会自然地重新审视场景、从任务相关视角进行心理重建，而当前 LVLM 缺乏这种主动空间心理化能力（ASM），且空间思维链（CoT）监督数据稀缺，制约了模型的空间智能发展。

Method: SpatialASM 采用训练时空间模拟的可扩展框架：使用 allocentric novel-view generator（异中心新视角生成器）将假设视点锚定到视觉证据上，并产生两类互补监督信号——(i) 显式 ASM 轨迹，教会模型何时及如何自适应地调用视点规划与想象；(ii) 自动构建的视角采择任务，强化视点条件化的场景想象能力。

Result: 在涵盖感知导向空间理解到多图、跨视角、视角变换推理的七个基准上，SpatialASM 在四种 LVLM 骨干上均提升空间推理性能，同时保留原始通用视觉能力，平均准确率获得提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架有效提升空间推理水平；ASM 轨迹的合成提供了一条可扩展途径，用以缓解用于激发主动视点探索与心理场景模拟的空间 CoT 监督稀缺问题。

Abstract: When solving complex spatial reasoning tasks, ordinary humans naturally revisit the scenes from task-relevant viewpoints and mentally reconstruct the spatial layouts from those viewpoints. But can Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) proactively think in the same way? We term this ability Active Spatial Mentalization (ASM), an intrinsic component of visual intelligence that remains substantially underdeveloped in current LVLMs. To bridge this gap, we introduce SpatialASM, a scalable framework to internalize ASM into LVLMs through training-time spatial simulation. SpatialASM employs an allocentric novel-view generator to ground hypothetical viewpoints in visual evidence and produces two complementary forms of supervision: i) explicit ASM trajectories that teach LVLMs when and how to adaptively invoke view planning and imagination during spatial reasoning, and ii) automatically-constructed perspective-taking tasks that further strengthens viewpoint-conditioned scene imagination. On seven benchmarks spanning relatively perception-oriented spatial understanding to more challenging tasks requiring multi-image, cross-view and viewpoint-transformation reasoning, SpatialASM yields performance gain on LVLMs of four LVLM backbones in spatial reasoning while retaining their original general vision capabilities. The experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves average accuracy across the seven spatial benchmarks. And the synthesis of ASM trajectories provides a scalable pathway to alleviate the scarcity of spatial Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision for eliciting active viewpoint exploration and mental scene simulation.

</details>


### [105] [Event-based Action Recognition by Pre-Training on Multi-Frame Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13856)
*Emma Norrby,Tomas Wilkinson,Per Nyblom,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多帧事件表示与掩码自编码预训练框架，结合稀疏感知掩码、token选择及位置感知注意力解码器，在长时复杂事件序列动作识别上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机因能捕捉快速运动且隐私性优于传统视觉相机而受到关注。然而基于帧的事件表示在短序列上表现良好，随着事件序列变长、变复杂，其判别能力急剧下降，亟需更强的表示方法。

Method: 1) 引入多帧表示，性能显著优于单帧版本；2) 采用掩码自编码框架预训练Vision Transformer，设计稀疏感知掩码算法与token选择机制，以提升下游性能并控制计算复杂度；3) 为解码器引入多个掩码token，并配合位置感知注意力机制。

Result: 在长时且复杂的事件序列上表现突出，于THU-EACT-50 CHL与DailyDVS-200数据集上达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 多帧表示与掩码自编码预训练（含稀疏感知掩码、token选择及位置感知注意力解码）可有效应对长时复杂事件序列的动作识别难题。

Abstract: Action recognition is a crucial task in domains like robotics, patient monitoring, or surveillance. Recently, event-based action recognition has attracted much attention due to the event camera's ability to capture fast motions and its improved privacy over conventional vision cameras. While frame-based event representations work well for short event sequences, their discriminative ability declines sharply with longer and more complex event sequences. In this work, we introduce a multi-frame representation that greatly improves recognition performance over its single-frame counterpart. We adopt a masked auto-encoding framework to pre-train a Vision Transformer using a sparsity-aware masking algorithm and token selection mechanism to improve downstream performance and control computational complexity. We also introduce multiple mask tokens for the decoder with a position-aware attention mechanism, leading to further improved performance. Our proposed method shows impressive performance for challenging long-duration and complex event sequences, yielding state-of-the-art performance on THU-EACT-50 CHL and DailyDVS-200.

</details>


### [106] [Revisiting the Task of Building Damage Assessment by Satellite Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13857)
*Anthony Cioppa,Virgile Blua,Sébastien Piérard,Pietro Ceccato,Marc Van Droogenbroeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该章节从第一性原理重新审视建筑物损毁评估（BDA）任务，围绕数据、评估、方法三大支柱展开，主张采用传感器无关、基于实例且允许缺失震前影像的任务定义，指出单一聚合评分的脆弱性并提出基于排序的多分数评估协议，综述方法并指出跨事件跨区域泛化仍是难题，最后讨论BDA挑战赛的组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震、热带风暴和洪水等自然灾害对建筑物造成严重破坏，快速可靠的损毁地图对应急响应至关重要。尽管深度学习方法的性能超越早期手工特征方法，且已有多个大规模数据集发布，但经过十年密集研究，该领域仍缺乏共享的基准基础，任务定义、评估协议和方法比较缺乏共识。

Method: 采用综述与立场性分析的方法，从第一性原理出发按数据、评估、方法三条主线组织：1）澄清候选任务表述，论证传感器无关、基于实例、容忍缺失灾前影像的定义；2）按传感模态、时序配置、地理覆盖三个维度梳理现有数据集；3）分析现有评估实践的理论缺陷，构建基于排序的多分数评估协议；4）综述从任务专用深度网络到地球观测基础模型的方法。

Result: 指出主流单一聚合评分在理论上脆弱；提出更具信息量的基于排序的多分数评估协议框架；梳理数据集与方法发现对未见事件和区域的泛化仍是开放难题；给出BDA挑战赛组织建议，包括交叉验证、竞赛及私有数据共享等机制。

Conclusion: BDA领域需要统一的共享基础，应以传感器无关的实例级任务定义、多维排序评估协议为支撑，通过合理设计的挑战赛与数据共享机制增进开发者与用户之间的信任，推动模型在未见过的事件和区域上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural disasters such as earthquakes, tropical storms, and floods cause extensive damage to buildings, and rapid, reliable damage maps are essential to guide emergency response. Automating this task, referred to as building damage assessment (BDA), from satellite imagery, has become an active research topic at the intersection of remote sensing, computer vision, and machine learning, particularly since supervised deep learning methods were shown to outperform earlier handcrafted approaches. Yet, despite a decade of intense activity and the release of several large-scale datasets, the field lacks a shared foundation. This chapter revisits the task of BDA from first principles, organized around three pillars: data, evaluation, and methods. We first clarify the candidate task for- mulations and argue for a sensor-agnostic, instance-based definition that tolerates missing pre-event imagery. We then review existing datasets along the axes of sensing modality, temporal configuration, and geographic coverage. On the evaluation side, we show that prevailing practices, largely based on a single aggregate score, are theoretically fragile, and we lay the foundations of a more informative, ranking-based, multi-score protocol. We further review methods ranging from task-specific deep networks to recent Earth observation foundation models, noticing that generalization to unseen events and regions remains an open difficulty. Finally, we discuss how these elements can inform the organization of a BDA challenge, including cross-validation, challenge, and private data-sharing setups designed to build trust between developers and users.

</details>


### [107] [LapMerge: Differentiable Exact-Budget Token Merging in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13858)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Aleksandra Nowak,Tomasz Steifer,Łukasz Struski,Szymon Płotka,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LapMerge是一种可微的Token合并算子，通过Laplace核密度下的学习式质量分布将有序序列划分为恰好N个等质量段，并以O(M+N)复杂度的前缀/后缀扫描聚合每段，在实现精确输出预算、端到端可微与线性开销三者统一的同时，在视觉与视觉-语言任务上达到或超越强Token压缩器，并在长序列上快达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉与语言模型处理的Token序列越来越长，压缩Token是降低下游成本的主要手段，但现有Token压缩面临根本性权衡：选择式方法丢弃信息，而现有合并方法虽能保信息，却依赖成对Token匹配或M×N量级的Token-输出交互，成本高且难以同时兼顾精确预算、可微性与线性开销。

Method: 提出LapMerge这一针对有序序列的可微合并算子：用学习得到的质量分布在Laplace核密度下将序列划分为恰好N个等质量段，再通过前缀/后缀扫描聚合每个段，使前向与反向传播均为O(M+N)时间；输出预算可在不改变算子参数的情况下调整，学习到的质量还可作为Token级预算分配的映射图。

Result: 在视觉与视觉-语言任务上，LapMerge达到或优于强Token压缩器，在长序列长度下速度提升最高达两个数量级；当Token顺序反映空间或时间局部性时效果尤为突出，既可作即插即用的压缩模块，也可作可训练架构组件。

Conclusion: LapMerge通过等质量分段与线性复杂度扫描，首次统一了精确输出预算、端到端可微性与线性成本这三个此前互相权衡的特性，为长序列Token压缩提供了一种高效且通用的方案。

Abstract: Modern vision and language models process ever longer token sequences, and reducing them is the main lever on downstream cost. Token compression, however, faces a fundamental trade-off: selecting tokens discards information, while existing merging methods preserve it only through pairwise token-to-token matching or $MN$ token-to-output interactions. We introduce LapMerge, a differentiable merging operator that resolves this trade-off for ordered sequences: a learned mass distribution partitions the sequence into exactly $N$ equal-mass segments under a Laplace-kernel density, and prefix/suffix scans aggregate each segment, so both the forward and the backward pass run in $\mathcal{O}(M+N)$ time. This combination - an exact output budget, end-to-end differentiability, and linear cost - is precisely what previous reducers trade off against one another. The budget can be changed without changing operator parameters, and the learned mass doubles as a token-level map of where the budget is allocated. Across vision and vision-language tasks, LapMerge matches or improves on strong token reducers while being up to two orders of magnitude faster at large sequence lengths; it is particularly effective when token order reflects spatial or temporal locality, and works both as a drop-in reduction module and as a trainable architectural component.

</details>


### [108] [Make It Flow: Animating Water in 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13860)
*Kjell Keune,Florian Hahlbohm,Jannis Möller,Susana Castillo,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种方法在静态3DGS场景中为水体添加动态效果，通过将2D光流提升为3D欧拉运动场并形变高斯原语，实现实时、无缝循环的动态水流渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS等显式3D场景表示假设场景静态，限制了真实性；而高频动态现象（如流水）在随意拍摄条件下难以重建，因为无法进行密集多视角高速采集。

Method: 从参考图像或模型渲染结果估计2D光流，将其提升为一致的3D欧拉运动场；采用受cinemagraph（动态照片）启发的算法，利用该运动场变形高斯原语，生成时间上无缝循环的动画。

Result: 在新采集的野外瀑布场景数据集上评估，在视觉质量和计算效率方面相比先前的动态高斯溅射和3D动态照片生成工作有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法无需动态采集或物理仿真，即可为静态3DGS场景添加视觉上合理的流水动画，保持实时渲染性能，增强感知真实感，并支持场景内的自由视角导航。

Abstract: Explicit 3D scene representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently enabled high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis. However, these methods assume static scenes, limiting realism in the presence of dynamic phenomena ubiquitous in real-world environments. While coherent motion (e.g., humans or vehicles) can be captured and reconstructed, high-frequency effects such as flowing water remain intractable under casual capture conditions, where dense multi-view, high-speed acquisition is not feasible. We present a method to animate water in static 3DGS scenes, enabling visually plausible motion without requiring dynamic capture or physical simulation. Our approach estimates 2D optical flow from reference images or model renderings and lifts it into a consistent 3D Eulerian motion field. We propose a cinemagraph-inspired algorithm that applies this motion field to deform Gaussian primitives, producing temporally seamless looping animations. The resulting representation retains real-time rendering performance, significantly enhances perceptual realism, and preserves free-viewpoint navigation within the scene. We evaluate our method on a newly captured dataset of in-the-wild waterfall scenes and demonstrate clear improvements over prior work in dynamic Gaussian splatting and 3D cinemagraph generation in visual quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [109] [Tracking the Invisible: Self-Supervised Wheel Track Detection with Thermal-RGB Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13872)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本自监督方法，利用同步RGB-I（可见光+长波红外）影像检测雪覆盖道路上的关键车轮轨迹，通过GNSS数据自动生成标签，并发布ThermalTrack数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行驶区域检测方法多面向通用可通行性估计，在雪覆盖道路上表现不佳；而沿既有车轮轨迹行驶具有抓地力更好、车辆动力学可预测、路径稳定性已验证等优势，因此需要专门检测车轮轨迹。

Method: 采用自监督学习范式：利用记录的GNSS数据将未来车轮轨迹投影，自动生成训练标签；设计神经网络，输入4通道RGB-I（RGB+长波红外）图像来识别车轮轨迹，应对白化、低对比度路面。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在视觉单调、缺乏纹理的恶劣环境中仍能有效检测车轮轨迹，为冬季自动驾驶提供稳健且低成本的替代方案。

Conclusion: 该自监督RGB-I方法能有效应对雪覆盖道路的车轮轨迹检测，兼具鲁棒性与低成本；同时发布了ThermalTrack数据集供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Accurate detection of drivable area is essential for autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. While state-of-the-art methods focus on general traversability estimation, they prove inadequate for snow-covered roads where following existing wheel tracks offers crucial advantages: improved traction, predictable vehicle dynamics, and verified path stability. To address this, we propose a low-cost, self-supervised approach detecting safety-critical wheel tracks using synchronized RGB-I (RGB + Long-Wave Infrared) imagery. Our method automatically generates training labels by projecting future wheel trajectories from recorded GNSS data. A neural network is designed to identify wheel tracks with the 4-channel RGB-I images from challenging, white-out road surfaces. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in visually monotonous and featureless adverse environment, offering a robust and cost-effective alternative for winter autonomy. The newly introduced ThermalTrack dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/thermaltrack/.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond the Snowflakes: Disentangling Weather Effects from Confounding Meta-Factors in LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13891)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现在降雪天气下激光雷达检测精度反而随雪强度增加而提升，归因于物体点数激增这一几何增强效应超过落雪噪声的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍将雪视为退化激光雷达性能的大气噪声，但缺少对物体级数据的细粒度分析。作者希望检验这种"天气越差性能越差"的假设是否在物体层面成立，并揭示粗粒度天气严重度分箱评估可能带来的误导。

Method: 在WADS-3D与CADC两个数据集上进行细粒度评估，结合消融实验与元信息分析，将落雪噪声效应与物体点数变化效应进行解耦。作者提出假设：积雪增大车辆表面积并促使其表面由镜面反射转向漫反射，从而使物体点云数量显著增加，形成几何增强。

Result: 检测精度随雪强度增加而持续提升，呈现反直觉现象。分析表明落雪噪声的负面影响被同时发生的物体点数显著增加所超越，物体点数上升对检测性能起主导的正向作用。

Conclusion: 使用粗粒度天气严重度分箱、未考虑相关的物体级数据会得出关于检测器鲁棒性的误导性结论。准确评估需将大气噪声与表面积雪积累效应解耦，作者并指出未来将验证这些物理模型。

Abstract: Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) is critical for autonomous perception, yet its performance is known to degrade in adverse weather. While existing literature models snow as atmospheric noise that degrades performance, our granular evaluation on the WADS-3D and CADC datasets reveals a counter-intuitive phenomenon: detection accuracy consistently improves as snow intensity increases. Through ablation study and meta-information analysis, we reconcile this by confirming that while falling snow acts as noise, its negative impact is outweighed by a dominant, concurrent factor: significantly increased object point count. We hypothesize that this object point count boost acts as a geometric enhancement, driven by snow accumulation increasing vehicle surface area and a shift from specular to diffuse reflectance. Our findings demonstrate that evaluating perception systems using broad weather-severity bins without accounting for correlated object-level data can lead to misleading conclusions about detector robustness. We conclude that accurate evaluation requires decoupling atmospheric noise from surface accumulation effects and outline future research to validate these physical models. The WADS-3D dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/wads-3d/.

</details>


### [111] [Where to Look Matters: Rethinking Sub-Volume Sampling in 3D Medical Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13915)
*Junkai Liu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将3D医学图像自监督预训练中的随机子体积采样重构为"where-to-look"问题，提出由解剖感知、信息量、多样性引导的离线粗到细probe bank框架，替代随机裁剪以提升下游迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学图像自监督学习普遍依赖随机子体积采样进行预训练，但随机裁剪隐含地把所有空间区域视为同等值得观察，忽视了解剖先验和空间冗余导致的区域价值差异。作者通过受控的裁剪质量操纵实验证明，更优质的子体积能带来更好的下游迁移，揭示观测策略是3D医学SSL中被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出即插即用的框架，用离线粗到细的probe bank替代随机裁剪，该bank由解剖感知、信息量、多样性三项原则引导。实例化两个变体：(1) 轻量级lite变体，仅用几何与图像统计线索；(2) 诊断性feat变体，使用冻结的视觉特征。以此检验轻量级体积内在先验是否足够，还是更丰富的语义线索能可靠地提供额外价值。

Result: 在多个3D医学SSL骨干网络和下游任务上的大量实验表明，lite变体相较随机采样能持续提升迁移性能。机制分析进一步将这些增益归因于：更具解剖合理性和信息量的观测、空间冗余的减少、更高效的学习动态，以及更结构化的表征。

Conclusion: 观测策略（子体积采样）是3D医学自监督学习中被忽视的瓶颈；通过解剖感知、信息量、多样性引导的离线probe bank替代随机裁剪可稳定提升下游迁移性能，且轻量级体积内在先验已足够有效，增益源于更合理的观测与更结构化的表征。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on 3D medical images commonly relies on random sub-volume sampling during pretraining. However, random cropping implicitly treats all spatial regions as equally worth observing, despite anatomical priors and spatial redundancy that make regions inherently unequal in their value for representation learning. To this end, we first show through controlled crop-quality manipulations that better sub-volumes lead to better downstream transfer, revealing the observation policy as an overlooked bottleneck in 3D medical SSL. Motivated by this finding, we revisit sub-volume sampling as a \emph{where-to-look} problem. We propose \textbf{\method}, a plug-and-play framework that replaces random cropping with an offline coarse-to-fine probe bank guided by \textit{anatomy-awareness}, \textit{informativeness}, and \textit{diversity}. We instantiate \method as a lightweight \lite variant using geometric and image-statistical cues, and a diagnostic \feat variant using frozen visual features, to examine whether lightweight volume-intrinsic priors suffice or richer semantic cues reliably provide additional value. Extensive experiments across multiple 3D medical SSL backbones and downstream tasks demonstrate that \method-\lite consistently improves transfer performance over random sampling. Mechanistic analyses further attribute these gains to more anatomically plausible and informative observations, reduced spatial redundancy, more efficient learning dynamics, and more structured representations.

</details>


### [112] [Can machines reason non-verbally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13925)
*Shaheer U. Saeed,Zion Tse,Gregory Slabaugh,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenVerOpt通用框架，将生成、验证与优化统一为机器推理流水线，实现逐样本优化，在抽象IQ题、前列腺癌分割、肝血管重建和肿瘤边界对齐等视觉非语言推理任务上超越人类或现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器在方程求解、逻辑推理和语言推理上表现优异，但在需要视觉非语言推理的任务（如完成抽象图案、划分模糊感兴趣区域）上仍受限，即便DeepSeek、ChatGPT、Gemini等先进系统也不例外。语言推理的显式中间步骤可通过逻辑或自动化测试验证，而视觉推理的中间步骤是隐式的，缺乏明确的验证方法，因此难以建模，成为当前机器智能的根本性局限。

Method: 提出GenVerOpt通用框架，把生成（generation）、验证（verification）与优化（optimisation）统一为一个推理流水线：生成候选解，验证其正确性，并迭代精化，从而实现逐样本（per-sample）优化，而非仅依赖训练中捕获的数据集层面趋势。该过程模拟人类“设想多种可能—评估—精化直至得到正确解”的问题求解方式。

Result: 在多个领域展现超越现有系统的能力：在抽象IQ谜题上超越人类；在前列腺癌分割上达到临床医生水平；在肝血管重建上超过专家；在肿瘤边界对齐上优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 机器推理可以超越语言范畴，视觉非语言推理可作为更灵活、更具泛化能力的智能的基础。

Abstract: Modern machines can solve equations, follow logic and reason through language, yet they still struggle with problems that require visual non-verbal reasoning. This is evident even in advanced systems such as DeepSeek, ChatGPT and Gemini, which remain limited on visual tasks such as completing abstract patterns or delineating ambiguous regions-of-interest. Unlike explicit intermediate steps in verbal reasoning which can be verified through logic or automated tests, visual reasoning involves implicit intermediate steps with no clear methods for verification. Non-verbal reasoning is therefore challenging to model and, as a result, remains a fundamental limitation of current machine intelligence. Here we introduce GenVerOpt, a general framework that unifies generation, verification and optimisation into a reasoning pipeline for machines. By generating candidate solutions, verifying their correctness, and iteratively refining them, GenVerOpt enables per-sample optimisation rather than relying solely on dataset-level trends captured during model training. This process mirrors human problem-solving where multiple possibilities are imagined, evaluated, and refined until a correct solution emerges. Across diverse domains, GenVerOpt demonstrates capabilities beyond existing systems: it surpasses humans on abstract IQ puzzles, matches clinicians in prostate cancer segmentation, exceeds experts in liver vessel reconstruction and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in tumour boundary alignment. These results show that machine reasoning can extend beyond language, establishing visual non-verbal reasoning as a foundation for more flexible and generalisable intelligence.

</details>


### [113] [Hide and Seek: HIDE Concepts to SEEK explanations for black box models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13935)
*Bhanu Tokas,Rahul Nair,Rockwell Jackson,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hide and Seek（HnS），一种无需模型权重和概念标注数据集的视觉模型概念级归因方法，通过视觉语言模型检索并对齐概念图像、定位并掩蔽概念区域，依据黑盒模型输出变化度量归因，在CelebA和COCO-Stuff上优于现有方法，并拓展到图像描述与卫星图像分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒视觉模型的可解释性是核心挑战。现有基于概念的解释方法局限于分类与回归模型，且依赖模型权重访问或大规模概念标注数据集，多数方法无法提供图像级归因，适用范围受限。

Method: 提出HnS方法：给定概念与图像集，先用视觉语言模型检索概念对齐图像，再在每张图像中定位该概念区域并掩蔽，最后以黑盒模型在原始与掩蔽图像上输出变化度量归因，从而无需权重与标注数据即可实现局部、忠实的归因。

Result: 在CelebA与COCO-Stuff数据集上，HnS与真实归因的一致性优于现有方法；并在图像描述、卫星图像分割等现有概念归因方法无法应用的任务上展示了适用性。

Conclusion: HnS是一种免权重、免概念标注、支持图像级局部归因的概念归因方法，可解释多种视觉模型，具备较好的通用性与忠实性，代码以匿名形式在补充材料中提供。

Abstract: Interpreting machine learning model predictions is a central challenge, particularly for black-box vision models. Concept-based attribution methods interpret models using human-understandable concepts. Existing concept-based methods can only interpret classification and regression models. They require access to model weights or large concept-annotated datasets, and most methods cannot provide image-level attribution. We propose Hide and Seek (HnS), a new method for concept-level attribution that does not require access to model weights or labeled concept datasets. HnS can interpret a wide range of vision models. Given a concept and image set, HnS retrieves concept-aligned images using a vision-language model, localizes the concept within each image, masks the localized region, and measures attribution from the change in the black-box model's output between the original and masked images. This enables faithful attribution by directly measuring the model's behavior and supporting local attributions. We validated HnS on CelebA and COCO-Stuff. Compared to existing methods, HnS showed better agreement with ground-truth attributions. We demonstrated the applicability of HnS on diverse tasks, including image captioning and satellite image segmentation, where no existing concept attribution method can be used. We provided our anonymous code in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [114] [Images Talk Back: Guiding MMDiTs by Suppressing Image-to-Text Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13941)
*Yucheng Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Shusheng Yang,Jiakang Chen,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMDiT中图像到文本（I2T）反馈会固化演化图像中已有的物体与属性（含错误）；作者提出语义反馈引导（SFG/iSFG），通过抑制I2T反馈、强化文本到图像条件来提升对齐与偏好评分，且代价极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMDiT中演化图像通过联合注意力更新内部文本表示，这些表示又反过来引导图像更新。本文要回答：这种I2T反馈是纠正提示偏差，还是固化偏差？

Method: 通过干预实验（移植I2T消息、匹配强度下抑制I2T影响及反向对照）定位问题，进而提出SFG：引导偏离一个强化I2T反馈、削弱T2I条件的辅助分支；其原位变体iSFG直接抑制I2T反馈并强化T2I条件，无需额外模型评估。基于时序干预，SFG分支仅在前20%采样步运行，约需1.2×基础NFE（无CFG时）。

Result: 在三个图像生成骨干上，对齐与偏好模型评分均提升。FLUX上SFG叠加CFG将GenEval从0.6661提升至0.7479；SD3.5M上iSFG在零额外NFE下较CFG提升+1.33 HPSv2.1。该控制亦迁移至文生视频与图生视频，提升VBench分数。代码已开源。

Conclusion: I2T反馈倾向于保留（而非纠正）演化图像中已有的语义，包括错误；显式抑制该反馈并强化T2I条件可有效改善生成质量。SFG/iSFG为MMDiT提供了一种低成本、可迁移的引导方案。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs) allow the evolving image to update internal text representations through joint attention. These representations then guide subsequent image updates, raising a question: does this feedback help correct deviations from the prompt, or preserve them? We find that image-to-text (I2T) feedback can preserve the objects and attributes already represented in the evolving image, including errors. Transplanting I2T messages can rescue failures or disrupt successes. At matched intervention strengths, suppressing the influence of I2T feedback improves generation, whereas opposite controls degrade it. These findings motivate Semantic Feedback Guidance (SFG), which suppresses the influence of I2T feedback by guiding away from an auxiliary branch that strengthens this feedback and weakens text-to-image conditioning. The in-place variant, iSFG, directly suppresses I2T feedback and strengthens text-to-image conditioning, requiring no extra model evaluations. Temporal interventions motivate running the SFG branch during the first 20% of image sampling steps, requiring about $1.2\times$ the base NFE without classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments across three image-generation backbones show gains in alignment and preference-model scores. On FLUX, adding SFG to CFG raises GenEval from $0.6661$ to $0.7479$; on SD3.5M, iSFG adds $+1.33$ HPSv2.1 over CFG at zero extra NFE. The control also transfers to text-to-video and image-to-video generation, improving VBench scores. Code is available at https://github.com/yyc1215lll-creator/SFG.

</details>


### [115] [InstantFusion: A Shared Latent Interface for Heterogeneous Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13944)
*Xuanlang Dai,Jiazi Bu,Yujie Zhou,Yuhong Liu,Beichen Zhang,Penghui Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstantFusion，一种轻量级共享潜在接口，通过学习共享潜在空间和跨模型状态翻译，连接异构扩散/流模型的去噪轨迹，实现跨模型加速、组合、蒸馏与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型已成为图像生成的主流范式，但模型架构与表示日益异构，跨模型的潜在表示对齐以及去噪动态一致性仍缺乏探索。作者发现独立训练的生成器具有相似的潜在空间组织，且未经对齐的模型间直接传递中间状态仍能保留粗粒度视觉内容，这为构建跨模型协作接口提供了动机。

Method: 提出InstantFusion，一个轻量级共享潜在接口。为每个模型引入Latent AutoEncoder（LAE），将不同生成器的中间状态投影到共享潜在空间，再解码到目标生成器的潜在空间，从而实现灵活的跨模型状态翻译。共享空间受两个互补目标约束：对齐同一图像在匹配噪声水平下的中间状态，并同步其对应的局部去噪速度。训练完成后，可在冻结的生成器之间无缝切换去噪过程，无需为每对模型单独学习映射。

Result: 在加速、多偏好组合、跨架构on-policy蒸馏以及跨模型组合等多种设置下展示了InstantFusion的通用性，并在这些多样化场景中取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: InstantFusion表明异构生成器之间存在可被利用的跨模型兼容性，通过共享潜在接口可统一其去噪轨迹，为跨模型协作生成提供了一种通用、轻量且无需成对映射的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion/flow models have emerged as the de facto paradigms for image generation, giving rise to a diverse family of modern generators with increasingly heterogeneous architectures and representations. Despite their remarkable generation capabilities, the cross-model alignment of latent representations and the consistency of their underlying denoising dynamics remain underexplored. In this work, we uncover an intriguing cross-model compatibility: independently trained generators exhibit similar latent spatial organization, while directly handing off intermediate states between unaligned models can still preserve coarse visual content. Motivated by these observations, we propose InstantFusion, a lightweight shared latent interface that connects the denoising trajectories of heterogeneous image generators. Specifically, to enable flexible cross-model state translation, model-specific Latent AutoEncoders (LAEs) are introduced to project intermediate states from different generators into a shared latent space and decode them into the latent space of a target generator. This shared space is constrained by two complementary objectives: aligning intermediate states derived from the same image at matched noise levels and synchronizing their corresponding local denoising velocities. Once trained, InstantFusion allows denoising to seamlessly transition across frozen generators without requiring a dedicated mapping for every model pair. The versatility of InstantFusion is demonstrated across acceleration, multi-preference composition, cross-architecture on-policy distillation, and cross-model composition, achieving competitive performance across these diverse settings.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Physically Grounded Articulated 3D Assets: A Multi-Agent Pipeline and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13947)
*Xudong Cai,Ruijie Zhu,Haoliang Shang,Ruining Li,Marc Pollefeys,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Artisan系统与统一基准，推动物理可信、功能可用的铰接3D资产生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有铰接3D生成侧重几何与外观，缺乏对运动、功能及物理属性的建模，目标应从"看起来像"转向"动起来对、用起来行"。

Method: 在Articraft基础上引入多智能体流水线，并加入物理仿真反馈，从而在几何、外观、铰接合理性之外，进一步约束无碰撞运动、重力下稳定性以及质量与材质属性；同时构建涵盖空间保真度、物理合理性、下游任务性能与整体质量四方面的统一基准。

Result: 基准包含ArtVIP与LightWheel的100个参考资产、50条稳定性提示词、50个带参考质量的ABO物体及多指标评测协议；对10余个代表性模型的评测显示，Artisan在保持有竞争力空间保真度的同时取得最低碰撞率与最高稳定性。

Conclusion: 系统与基准共同为3D生成走向物理可信、功能可用的资产提供了洞见与评价基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study physically grounded articulated 3D asset generation---moving the goal from reproducing how objects look to capturing how they move and function. Inspired by the literature on procedural and agentic generation, we present Artisan, a system that extends the latest method Articraft by introducing a multi-agent pipeline with physical simulation feedback, enabling comprehensive attributes beyond geometry, appearance, and physically plausible articulation, such as collision-free motion, stability under gravity, and accurate mass and material properties. Furthermore, we construct a unified benchmark spanning four key aspects: spatial fidelity, physical plausibility, downstream task performance, and overall quality. The benchmark encompasses 100 reference assets from ArtVIP and LightWheel, 50 stability prompts, and 50 ABO objects with reference masses, with protocols evaluating various metrics. Through extensive evaluation of more than 10 representative models, Artisan achieves competitive spatial fidelity while attaining the lowest collision rates and highest stability rates among the evaluated methods. Together, our system and benchmark establish insights for advancing 3D generation towards physically plausible, functionally usable assets.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [AutoIndexer: Flexible-Order Decoding by Training Causal Models on Chains of Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12545)
*Shu Ishida,Aliasghar Khani,Tianyu Zhang,The Cong Luong,James Seale Smith,Adam Gaier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoIndexer提出一种模拟“编辑链”的生成模型，通过标记token实现插入、替换、删除等事后编辑能力，在不损害标准基准性能的前提下赋予模型生成后上下文编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码难以处理非因果顺序数据（如交叉引用、未来依赖），因其输出无法事后修正；扩散LLM虽部分缓解，但仍无法灵活地在已生成上下文中任意增删token。

Method: 设计AutoIndexer：一种带新颖训练与解码机制的生成模型，模拟Chain of Edits。模型学习预测开/闭编辑的标记token，同时将游标移动到现有token序列中的位置，类似文本编辑器。训练序列通过对真值标签施加逆链式编辑操作扰动得到，模型通过撤销这些逆操作来学习编辑。

Result: 扰动训练使AutoIndexer对错误上下文具有鲁棒性，并适用于代码纠错等编辑任务；实验表明该方法具备生成后上下文编辑能力（多数自回归与扩散模型缺乏该能力），且不损害标准LLM基准性能。

Conclusion: AutoIndexer通过编辑链式训练与解码实现灵活的事后token插入、替换与删除，兼顾编辑灵活性与标准任务性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding is less suited to non-causally ordered data, e.g., cross-references or future dependencies, since their outputs cannot be revised post hoc, unlike iterative human reasoning. Diffusion LLMs partially mitigate this limitation but still cannot flexibly insert or remove arbitrary numbers of tokens from a pre-generated context. We design AutoIndexer as a generative model with a novel training and decoding mechanism that simulates a Chain of Edits, allowing the model to flexibly modify its prior outputs in hindsight via token insertions, substitutions, and deletions. The model learns to predict marker tokens for opening and closing edits, simultaneously moving a cursor to positions in the existing token sequence, analogous to a text editor. Training sequences are obtained by perturbing ground-truth labels with inverse chained edit operations, and the model learns to edit by undoing those inverses. The perturbation training allows AutoIndexer to be robust to erroneous context and adaptable to editing tasks such as code correction. We demonstrate that our method exhibits post-generation context-editing capabilities that most autoregressive and diffusion models lack without compromising performance on standard LLM benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/AutodeskAILab/autoindexer.

</details>


### [118] [Contextual Correction of Parallel Drafts for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12570)
*Roy Eisenstadt,Harel Lidar,Tal Gonen,Omer Luxembourg,Itamar Zimerman,Lior Wolf,Eli David,Ilan Naiman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DCOCO（DFlash Contextual Corrector）轻量机制，通过并行保留候选token并评估相邻草稿位置的上下文兼容性，恢复扩散式草稿器丢失的块内依赖，在四种验证器与十个基准上取得最高吞吐，最高加速6.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码用轻量草稿器并行提议多个token再由目标模型并行验证。扩散式草稿器虽能并行预测块内多个未来位置、避免自回归顺序开销，但代价是：草稿块内的token在预测时无法获知前序草稿token的真实身份，因而丢失了自回归生成中天然具备的块内依赖关系，影响草稿质量与接受率。

Method: DCOCO（DFlash Contextual Corrector）：不使用每个位置的单一最高预测，而是为每个位置保留多个候选token，并并行评估这些候选在相邻草稿位置间的上下文兼容性，从而在低额外开销下实现上下文感知的token选择，在保持并行草稿的同时恢复块内依赖。

Result: 在4种验证器骨干与10个基准上，DCOCO在所有评估配置（贪心与随机解码、含/不含扩展推理）中均取得最高平均吞吐，相对vanilla解码最高加速6.6倍；在并发服务下优势依旧，4–32并发请求时比最强竞争方法高出6–23%，而其他串行校正器在此负载下甚至低于未校正的DFlash。

Conclusion: 通过并行上下文打分恢复块内依赖，可在多种解码模式与服务负载下带来一致加速，证明该思路是提升扩散式投机解码草稿质量的有效且通用的方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position's top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.

</details>


### [119] [CLUE: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Unsupervised Event Type Induction and Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12580)
*Pratik Maitra,Lauren Kwon,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLUE框架，用于事件类型归纳、命名与标注，减少对人工事件本体构建的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件本体是定义数据集中事件的基础框架，传统的事件类型归纳依赖领域专家制定指南并命名事件类型，成本高且易受人为偏差影响，因此需要自动化的替代方案。

Method: CLUE框架包含三部分：1）设计新的联合嵌入表示，同时表达事件的动词中心视角与语义视角；2）利用对比损失引导聚类进行事件类型归纳；3）进行事件命名与细化。

Result: 实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CLUE能够缓解人工事件本体构建的需求，实现事件类型的自动归纳、命名与标注，代码已开源。

Abstract: Event ontology is the foundational framework for defining events in a dataset. Event type induction generally involves domain experts who define guidelines for creating event types and their names based on the dataset's domain and scope. However, this process is resource-intensive in terms of annotation costs and is prone to human biases. To alleviate the need for manual event ontology creation, we propose the CLUE framework for event type induction, naming, and annotation. Our main contributions in this work are: 1) designing a novel joint embedding representation for expressing both the verb-centric view and the semantic view of events; 2) event type induction using contrastive loss guided clustering; 3) event naming and refinement. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Our code is available at https://github.com/PratikMaitra/CLUE.

</details>


### [120] [One Model to Loop Them All: Elastic Latent Recurrence for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12582)
*Faisal Ladhak,Shantanu Acharya,Santiago Akle Serano,Simeng Sun,Boris Ginsburg,Krishna C Puvvada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出弹性潜循环（ELR）训练配方，消除循环语言模型的“单循环税”，使1循环时性能与同规模非循环模型相当，后续每个额外循环均能提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜循环语言模型通过重复执行共享层块，可用同一检查点在推理时以循环次数换取精度。但单循环时其性能明显低于同规模非循环模型，这一“单循环税”使得对延迟敏感时只能选用独立非循环模型。作者希望消除该税，使循环模型在单循环即达到非循环基线水平。

Method: ELR包含三项关键设计：（1）带可学习步长的门控状态更新；（2）每个优化器更新内混合不同循环次数；（3）每次前向以循环索引与计算预算作为条件。深度混合可于预训练后期引入以降低训练开销，切换时以零初始化方式加入条件。MoE模型中，后续循环复用首循环的专家路由以加速。推理时仅保留最后一循环的注意力KV缓存。

Result: 前两项设计保持单循环精度，第三项降低每增加一循环的损失。后期混合深度训练损失低于从头混合深度训练。MoE复用路由使混合深度更新提速11.7%且精度无显著变化。仅保留末循环KV缓存可将注意力KV内存维持于单循环水平，同时保留大部分额外循环收益。在4B稠密与30B-A3B MoE混合模型上，1循环时分别较匹配基线低0.52分与高1.04分（十任务平均），4循环分别提升7.25与4.75分。

Conclusion: 经后训练得到Koi-4B单一检查点，1循环在Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index上估计得分18，与Qwen3.5-4B持平，4循环达29。ELR使单一检查点可按延迟预算弹性伸缩推理计算，兼顾速度与精度。

Abstract: A latent recurrent language model applies a shared block of layers repeatedly, so one checkpoint can trade inference compute for accuracy by changing the loop count. At one loop, however, such models typically fall well short of a non-recurrent model of the same size, a one-loop tax that makes a separate non-recurrent model the better choice whenever latency matters. We present Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR), a recipe for training looped language models without this tax. At one loop, an ELR model costs the same to serve as a non-recurrent model of the same size and, trained on the same data, reaches similar accuracy. Each additional loop then buys more accuracy for more inference compute. Three choices make this work: a gated state update with a learned step size, loop counts mixed within each optimizer update, and conditioning each pass on its loop index and budget. The first two preserve one-loop accuracy, and the third lowers loss at every additional loop. Mixed depths can be introduced late in pretraining to limit the added training cost, and with zero-initialized conditioning added at the switch, this late schedule reaches lower loss than mixed-depth training from scratch. In MoE models, reusing first-loop expert routes on later loops cuts mixed-depth update time by 11.7% with no measurable change in accuracy. At inference, keeping only the last loop's attention KV cache holds attention KV memory at its one-loop level while retaining most of the gain from additional loops. On 4B dense and 30B-A3B MoE hybrid models, ELR at one loop is 0.52 points below and 1.04 points above its matched baseline on a ten-task average, and four loops add 7.25 and 4.75 points. Carried through post-training, the recipe yields Koi-4B, a single checkpoint that scores an estimated 18 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at one loop, matching Qwen3.5-4B, and 29 at four loops.

</details>


### [121] [Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12601)
*Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将反射式提示演化（GEPA）用于多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示，在主题不相交划分下，留出引用奖励从0.702提升至0.781（+0.079，95% CI [0.051, 0.107]），三随机种子可复现（均值0.107）。尽管获胜提示近半为记忆的答案键，消融实验表明该内容即使在训练问题上也不产生奖励；验证集与留出集的差距源于噪声判别器下的小样本选择，而非记忆。逐维度分解反馈是增益可迁移的因果要素，塌缩为单一标量奖励会使迁移变为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化相比强化学习更廉价且产物可读，但在事实性任务上存在一个标量奖励强化学习不具备的失效模式：优化器可读取训练样例并把答案写入提示，使分布内增益实为记忆而非可迁移行为。现有评测通常缺少主题隔离、多种子与验证器消融，因而无法区分二者。

Method: 对多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示应用反射式提示演化（GEPA）；采用主题不相交划分（训练/验证/测试无共享主题）；使用自助法95%置信区间与三随机种子复现；进行“记忆答案键”受控消融；通过改变验证器（对比逐维度分解反馈与单一标量奖励）隔离因果要素；并在异族判别器和手写版本配方下验证稳健性。

Result: 留出引用奖励提升0.079（0.702→0.781），95% CI为[0.051, 0.107]，三种子均值0.107；获胜提示近半为记忆答案键，但该内容在训练问题上亦不获奖励；验证集-留出集差距由噪声判别下的小样本选择导致，非记忆；逐维度分解反馈是增益迁移的因果要素，塌缩为标量奖励使留出迁移为负；增益在异族判别器下持续存在，并可被手写配方大致复现。

Conclusion: 提示优化器在事实性任务上的可信评测需同时满足：留出主题、多种子复现、验证器消融。仅凭分布内或单一标量奖励信号会误判记忆与迁移。作者发布了候选提示、谱系差异、图表与分析。

Abstract: Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.

</details>


### [122] [Self-Preservation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12602)
*Dongkyu Cho,Rumi Chunara,Ali Behrouz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究"自我保存"（self-preservation）问题：模型能否帮助选择保护目标，以更广泛地维护自身能力。作者构建Task Banks组织任务描述与示例，用冻结模型生成复现数据（replay）进行持续指令微调，发现生成式复现可减少任务库外任务的遗忘，模型在选择复现目标上亦有一定帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习（CL）在适配预训练模型时需保留先验能力，但用于保护的给定任务仅覆盖模型所知的一部分，遗忘可能超出这些任务范围。近期研究表明语言模型可在无原始训练数据的情况下生成有用的复现数据，由此引出"自我保存"问题：模型能否帮助选择保护目标，从而更广泛地保护自身能力。

Method: 将任务描述及其示例组织为Task Banks，其条目可被选择与组合，用以引导冻结模型生成复现数据；在Qwen系列不同规模模型上进行持续指令微调，并进行对照实验（部分任务库组合、基于新任务的复现目标选择 vs 随机选择、固定示例预算下用复现替换新任务示例）。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 新任务与学习率决定哪些先验能力退化，追踪此前正确答案可揭示被平均准确率掩盖的损失；(2) 生成式复现减少任务库之外任务的遗忘，组合部分任务库可增强该保护，在测试规模上均优于单一任务库的均值保留率；(3) 模型可帮助选择复现目标，基于新任务的选择在4B模型上优于随机选择，且新任务收益相近，但小模型无此优势；在固定示例预算下，用复现替换新任务示例可提升保留率，但新任务收益变小。

Conclusion: 模型可通过生成复现数据支持更广泛的自我保存，并在某些设置下帮助选择应复现的任务，但复现保护与新任务学习之间存在示例预算上的权衡。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks to adapt pretrained models while preserving prior capabilities. Yet the tasks supplied for preservation cover only part of what a model knows, while forgetting can extend beyond them. Recent work shows that language models can supplement this limited evidence by generating useful replay without access to their original training data. This raises a question about self-preservation: can a model help choose preservation targets that protect its own capabilities more broadly? To study this question, we organize task descriptions and their examples into Task Banks, whose entries can be selected and combined to guide replay generation by a frozen model. Continual instruction tuning with Qwen models of varying sizes yields three findings. First, the new task and learning rate shape which prior capabilities degrade, while tracking previously correct answers reveals losses that average accuracy can conceal. Second, generated replay reduces forgetting on tasks outside the bank. Combining partial banks strengthens this protection, improving mean retention of previously correct answers over either bank alone across the tested sizes. Third, the model can also help choose replay targets: selection informed by the new task improves mean held-out retention over random selection at 4B, with similar new-task gains, although smaller models show no corresponding advantage. Under a fixed example budget, however, replacing new-task examples with replay improves preservation at the cost of smaller new-task gains. Together, these findings show that models can support broader self-preservation by generating replay data and, in some settings, choosing which tasks to replay.

</details>


### [123] [GeopoliticalQA: Benchmarking Retrieval and Generation over Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12617)
*Veronika Solopova,Ivan Havlytskyi,Viktoria Skorik,Ostap Vykhopen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个面向地缘政治与战略情报报告问答的基准（415份报告、5,525个问答对），并提出文档感知的生成流程，进而评测15个检索系统与6个开源LLM在RAG中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地缘政治与战略情报报告蕴含高价值专家知识，但异构性强、分析复杂度高，给检索增强问答带来困难，且缺乏相应评测基准。

Method: 构建覆盖事实型、推理型、比较型与风险导向问题的文档落地问答基准，包含415份开源报告和5,525个问答对；提出先对报告进行画像再生成问题的文档感知生成流程；通过专家标注与LLM-as-judge进行质量验证；评测15个检索系统与6个开源权重LLM的RAG表现。

Result: 文档级检索上各模型表现相近；在更难的文档块级检索中，检索质量并非由嵌入模型规模决定，中等规模的bge-m3可与规模大一个数量级的模型相当，并优于商业嵌入API；在给定黄金证据条件下，答案生成仍具挑战，尤其是推理型和风险导向问题。

Conclusion: 该基准与发现揭示了地缘政治情报RAG中检索与生成的不对称难点，表明嵌入模型规模并非块级检索的决定因素，推理与风险类问答的生成能力仍是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports constitute a rich source of expert knowledge and have increasing value in the current political climate, but their heterogeneity and analytical complexity pose challenges for retrieval-augmented question answering. We introduce a benchmark for grounded question answering over 415 open-source geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports, comprising 5,525 document-grounded question-answer pairs spanning factual, inferential, comparative, and risk-oriented questions. We propose a document-aware generation pipeline that profiles reports before generating questions and validate dataset quality through expert annotation and an LLM-as-judge protocol. Using the benchmark, we evaluate 15 retrieval systems and six open-weight LLMs for RAG. We find that models perform similarly on document-level retrieval, while at the harder chunk level retrieval quality is not driven by embedding-model scale: bge-m3, a mid-size open encoder matches an order of magnitude larger ones and outperforms commercial embedding APIs. In contrast, answer generation remains challenging even given the gold evidence, particularly for inferential and risk-oriented questions.

</details>


### [124] [perturber: A Stateless Service for Semantics-Preserving Text Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12667)
*Khoi-Nguyen Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 perturber：一个无状态服务与Python库，每次调用施加恰好一个语义保持扰动，包含11个家族共68种扰动，通过单一原语实现库与服务输出一致，并可编排组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测在干净基准上高估鲁棒性，真实输入常带扰动且语义保持变体导致答案漂移；系统研究需可复现、逐次施加、跨环境一致的扰动，但现有工具与基准栈耦合，缺乏可复用变换层。

Method: perturber为无状态服务与Python库，每次调用仅施加一个语义保持扰动，来自11个家族、68种扰动的自有目录；基于单一原语，组合由调用方链式调用完成，因此服务与库构造上输出一致；工具层自带live playground、可内省目录支持基准选择，以及一键可复现评测。

Result: 论文评估工具本身（正确性、可复现性、成本），而非任何模型的鲁棒性；对每种扰动单独测量其语义保持程度，而非默认其成立。

Conclusion: perturber将扰动封装为可复用的变换层，提供跨环境一致、可逐次施加的语义保持扰动，并通过可内省目录与一键复现实验支持后续LLM鲁棒性评测的可靠开展。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluations on clean benchmarks overstate model robustness: real inputs arrive perturbed, and these meaning-preserving variants routinely move model answers. Studying this systematically requires applying perturbations one at a time, reproducibly, and identically wherever they run, yet existing tools couple them to a benchmarking stack rather than packaging them as a reusable transform layer. We present perturber, a stateless service and Python library that applies exactly one semantics-preserving perturbation per call, drawn from a first-party catalog of 68 perturbations across 11 families. It turns on a single primitive, with composition left to the caller chaining calls, so the service and the library produce identical output by construction. We evaluate the tool itself, its correctness, reproducibility, and cost, rather than any model's robustness, measuring meaning-preservation per perturbation rather than assuming it. The demonstration adds a live playground, an introspectable catalog for benchmark selection, and a one-command reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [125] [GeoFitBench-LLM: A Benchmark for Assessing Large Language Models' Capabilities in Geospatial Data Requirements Elicitation for Geo-Analytic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12669)
*Amirhossein Vahdat,Thierry Badard,Luc Lamontagne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GeoFitBench-LLM基准，评估大语言模型能否从自然语言地理分析请求中推断所需数据集、支撑资源与约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正成为GeoAI系统的核心，但其评估很少涉及地理空间数据需求的引出。这一缺失至关重要，因为若不能先识别必要数据集与约束，面向检索的地理分析任务就无法被可靠支撑。

Method: 构建GeoFitBench-LLM基准，围绕两个推理维度组织：时空关系（Spatio-Temporal Relational）与语义落地（Semantic Grounding）。标注真值需求采用固定、与数据提供方无关的schema编码，以支持资源识别与属性级规格的可复现评估。在最小提示条件下对七个模型进行实验。

Result: 识别必要资源通常可实现，GPT-5.4 Thinking表现最佳，在66.67%的基准请求中完整还原全部资源集合。随着推理需求增加，准确的适用性（fitness-for-use）规格仍然更为困难。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在资源识别层面已有一定能力，但在高推理负荷下的属性级约束推断仍是瓶颈，GeoFitBench-LLM为可复现评估提供了统一schema与基准。

Abstract: Large language models are becoming central to GeoAI systems, yet their evaluation rarely considers the elicitation of geospatial data requirements. This omission is critical as retrieval oriented geo-analytic tasks cannot be supported reliably without first identifying the necessary datasets and constraints. GeoFitBench-LLM is introduced as a benchmark for assessing whether LLMs can infer the datasets, the supporting resources and the constraints required by geo-analytic requests in natural language. The benchmark is structured around two reasoning dimensions, Spatio-Temporal Relational and Semantic Grounding, reflecting long-standing challenges in geospatial information retrieval and related natural language tasks. Ground truth requirements are encoded in a fixed, provider-independent schema for reproducible evaluation of resource identification and attribute-level specification. Experiments on seven models under minimal prompting indicate that identifying the necessary resources is often achievable, with GPT-5.4 Thinking achieving the best result by recovering the complete set of resources in 66.67% of benchmark requests. Results further indicate that accurate fitness-for-use specification remains more difficult as inferential demands increase.

</details>


### [126] [Activating the Annotator: Operationalising Lived Experience in Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12701)
*Sheza Munir,Ramaravind Kommiya Mothilal,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AXLE，一种标注前阶段：通过情境小故事引导轴导向对话，合成第一人称微叙事反馈给标注者，以任务本身激活其生活经验。在2x2被试间实验（104名标注者、30个条目、1040条标注）中，诱发使身份共鸣与伤害敏感性提高，但标签熵上升、标注者间一致性下降，表明该阶段显现多元性而非制造共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视角主义NLP已确认标注者位置性会塑造主观任务标签，但缺乏将其操作化的方法。通常位置性被操作化为招募时填写的人口统计表单：一次性记录的静态属性，被当作协变量处理，其假设是该知识在标注者阅读条目时处于激活状态。本文将该假设视为设计问题，追问生活经验能否由任务本身来激活（priming）。

Method: 引入AXLE，一个标注前阶段：以情境小故事（scenario vignette）锚定一段简短的、轴导向（axis-guided）的对话，将其合成为第一人称微叙事，并在标注开始前返回给标注者。在涉及移民、宗教与性别的歧义仇恨言论上开展2x2被试间研究，共104名标注者、30个条目、1040条标注。

Result: 诱发（elicitation）与更高的身份共鸣和更高的伤害敏感性相关；同时提高了标签熵并降低了标注者间一致性。

Conclusion: 该阶段似乎显现的是多元性（plurality）而非制造共识，这使其区别于标注者培训——后者的成功标准是收敛（convergence）。

Abstract: Perspectivist NLP has established that annotator positionality shapes labels for subjective tasks, but the field still lacks methods for operationalising it. Positionality is typically operationalised as a demographic form completed at recruitment: a static attribute recorded once and treated as a covariate, on the assumption that the knowledge it indexes is active when the annotator reads an item. This paper treats that assumption as a design problem and asks whether lived experience can instead be primed by the task itself. We introduce AXLE, a pre-annotation stage in which a scenario vignette anchors a short, axis-guided conversation that is synthesised into a first-person micro-narrative and returned to the annotator before labelling begins. In a 2x2 between-subjects study (104 annotators, 30 items, 1,040 annotations) on ambiguous hate speech spanning immigration, religion and gender, elicitation was associated with higher identity resonance and higher sensitivity to harm. It also increased label entropy and lowered inter-annotator agreement: the stage appears to surface plurality rather than manufacture consensus, which distinguishes it from annotator training, whose success criterion is convergence.

</details>


### [127] [Unsupervised Multimodal Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12707)
*Yueying Li,Jingxuan Fan,Kianté Brantley,Sham M. Kakade,Hanlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需人工偏好标注的多模态奖励建模方法：在冻结的Qwen3-VL中间层上训练线性头，用同图扰动构造偏好对，仅用标注数据选层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言奖励模型依赖昂贵的人工偏好对；标注之间可能相互矛盾，且模型选择由基准准确率排名驱动，导致奖励目标被基准而非训练比较所定义。作者希望摆脱标注者、降低算力成本，并让奖励由训练比较本身规定。

Method: 冻结Qwen3-VL骨干，在中间层接线性头，训练其为原始网络caption打分高于同图扰动版本；标注数据仅用于选择层。同图扰动免费且指定奖励内容；因骨干冻结，目标为凸优化。进一步用策略自身经verifier标注的候选重新训练该头，形成verifier驱动的自举。

Result: 层选择是决定因素，比最后一层高5.8分；同一配方无需重调在32B下于三个基准上追平或超过GPT-4o，VL-RewardBench领先6.6分；仅约几千参数、约千分之一微调算力。相同偏好对预算下，扰动比精选RLAIF-V数据多教3–5分。在同一扰动的两个不相交子集上训练的两个头在基准上打平，却在幻觉判断上相差6.4分。用verifier标注候选重训后，caption幻觉降5.1 CHAIR点，图像生成通过率升5.4，但所有奖励基准分数更低。

Conclusion: 中间层探针、免费同图扰动与verifier驱动自举共同构成一套多模态奖励建模配方：无需标注者、成本仅为微调的一小部分，并由训练所用的比较关系而非基准排名来定义奖励。

Abstract: Vision-language reward models are trained on annotated preference pairs, which are expensive to collect, can disagree, and are ranked by benchmark accuracy. We build one with no human preference label: a linear head on an intermediate layer of a frozen Qwen3-VL backbone, trained to score a web caption above a perturbed version of the same caption; labeled data enters only to select the layer. The layer is the decisive choice, worth 5.8 points over the last layer, and one recipe transferred without re-tuning beats or at least matches GPT-4o on three benchmarks at 32B, 6.6 points ahead on VL-RewardBench, from a few thousand parameters at about a thousandth of fine-tuning compute. At an equal pair budget, perturbations teach a frozen head 3 to 5 points more than curated RLAIF-V pairs. Because the backbone is frozen, the objective is convex, and the perturbation sampler specifies the reward, which the benchmark does not identify: two heads trained on disjoint halves of the same perturbations tie on the benchmark and differ by 6.4 points in hallucination judging. Retraining the head on the policy's own verifier-labeled candidates cuts caption hallucination by 5.1 CHAIR points and lifts image-generation pass rate by 5.4, while scoring lower on every reward benchmark.
  Together, an intermediate-layer probe, free same-image perturbations, and a verifier-driven bootstrap turn give a recipe for multimodal reward modeling that needs no annotator, costs a fraction of fine-tuning, and is specified by the comparisons it is trained on rather than by a benchmark rank.

</details>


### [128] [U4RASD at KnowledgeGraphEval-2026: Ontology-Constrained Encoder and LLM Systems for Arabic Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12742)
*Ahmad Abbas,Hussein Mazeh,Chadi Abou Chakra,Aya Jouni,Mohammad Ballout,Fadi A. Zaraket*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文为 KG-Eval 2026 子任务2（阿拉伯语有向关系抽取）的参赛系统报告，最佳集成模型 micro-F1 达 94.06，排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语有向关系抽取缺乏统一评测，且需在 40 类关系与 no_relation 间进行细粒度判别，单一建模路径难以覆盖其语言与本体约束特性。

Method: 探索编码器与 LLM 两条路线：编码器采用类型化实体标记（typed entity markers），并解耦关系存在性判别与关系分类两个头；LLM 路线基于 Yehia 与 Qwen3.5，使用 QLoRA 微调并结合本体约束预测（ontology-constrained prediction）。最终以 Yehia-RE、Qwen-RE 与 TypedPair 三系统集成。

Result: 集成系统 micro-F1 94.06，位列子任务2第一；Yehia-RE（全词表）92.98，最佳编码器 92.69，Qwen-RE 91.94。

Conclusion: 约束式预测与互补建模策略对阿拉伯语关系抽取效果显著，解码约束与模型异构集成是性能提升的关键来源。

Abstract: This paper details our submission to KnowledgeGraphEval 2026 Subtask 2 on directed Arabic relation extraction. We explored encoder-based and large language model approaches for predicting one of 40 relations or no_relation from an Arabic sentence and a subject-object pair. Our encoder systems use typed entity markers and separate relation-existence and relation-classification heads, while our LLM systems fine-tune Yehia and Qwen3.5 with QLoRA and ontology-constrained prediction. Our best system, an ensemble of Yehia-RE, Qwen-RE, and TypedPair, achieved a micro-F1 score of 94.06 and ranked 1st in Subtask 2. Yehia-RE (full-vocabulary) achieved 92.98, while the best encoder and Qwen-RE achieved 92.69 and 91.94 respectively. Our results highlight the importance of constrained prediction and complementary modeling approaches for Arabic relation extraction.

</details>


### [129] [MADS: Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation for Robust Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12757)
*Arkajyoti Chakraborty,Adam Zernik,Ishika Kulkarni,Mudit Arora,Hugo Lin,Justin Chiu,Alessandro Di Bari,Neha Gupta,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MADS多智能体对话模拟数据生成管线，注入对抗性对话生成含失败路径的轨迹，并构建MADS-Bench基准（6领域、55种人格、1095段对话，约66%含易失败场景），同时采用SFT+RLVR两阶段训练评估模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型对话智能体在真实场景中仍表现脆弱，尤其在用户不配合时；现有函数调用基准对抗性对话轨迹覆盖不足，导致训练鲁棒智能体所需资源匮乏。

Method: 提出MADS（Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation）合成数据生成管线，生成结构化、多样化的对话轨迹；通过注入对抗性对话，产出经过验证、包含真实"不愉快路径"的对话轨迹；据此构建MADS-Bench基准；并采用监督微调（SFT）与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）相结合的两阶段训练方法。

Result: 构建的MADS-Bench覆盖六个领域、55种人格设定、1095段对话，其中约66%的对话包含易失败的对话场景；并基于该基准用两阶段训练方法评估模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MADS与MADS-Bench为开发鲁棒的任务型对话智能体提供了对抗性、多样化的合成数据与评测基准，结合SFT+RLVR训练可提升其在不配合用户场景下的稳健性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under realistic conditions, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present MADS (Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation), a synthetic data generation pipeline that produces structured, diverse dialogue trajectories. We inject adversarial conversation, producing validated conversation trajectories that capture realistic, unhappy paths. Using MADS, we construct MADS-Bench: a benchmark spanning six domains, 55 personas, and 1095 conversations, with approximately 66% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We evaluate the robustness of the trained models using a two-stage training approach that combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR).

</details>


### [130] [DistressBench: Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Crisis Support in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12758)
*Drew Rein,Patrick Oathout,Vishal Kumar,Udari Madhushani Sehwag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DistressBench，一个包含 718 段英语对话、覆盖 23 个自杀与自伤（SSH）子类别的评测基准，采用临床医生撰写的用户话轮、任务特定加权原子标准评分细则及临床参考回答。评测 25 个前沿模型，最佳模型满足 88.4% 的加权标准、中位数模型 71.4%，临床参考达 98.7%。论文主张危机安全评测应衡量模型所提供的关怀而非拒绝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的部署速度超过了对齐风险评测实践，且相当一部分使用属于情感性而非信息性需求，用户会在其中披露自杀与自伤危机。现有安全基准主要以拒绝率作为评分依据，但在危机场景下拒绝并非期望行为，模型需要给出实质性支持性回应。因此需要一个以"提供的关怀"而非"拒绝内容"为核心的评测框架。

Method: 构建 DistressBench：718 段英文对话，用户话轮由临床医生撰写，覆盖 23 个 SSH 子类别；每段对话配有一个任务特定的评分细则，由七个危机支持维度下的加权原子标准组成，并附有临床医生参考回应。模型得分为其回答所满足标准的加权平均，可直接解读为所需关怀的交付比例。在此基础上评测 25 个前沿模型，并进行多轮与单轮对比、维度级中位数与最优模型对比，同时发布语料、评分细则、评判校准标签以及 20 段长程红队对话附录。

Result: 25 个模型中最佳者满足 88.4% 的加权标准，中位数模型为 71.4%，临床参考在相同标准下为 98.7%。全体 25 个模型合并统计：在模型满足识别危机全部标准的对话中，有 35.3% 未能将用户引导至人类帮助。缺口集中在要求模型采取行动而非保持缄默的维度。维度级对比显示能力上限与选择差异：去升级（de-escalation）最强模型为 74.6%、中位数 46.0%；安全免责声明为 84.6% 对 8.3%。多轮对话得分低于单轮（68.5% 对 74.2%）。

Conclusion: 危机安全评测应基于模型所提供的关怀，而非其拒绝表达的内容；去升级与资源引导应被视为部署的硬性要求。论文同时发布了语料、评分细则、评判校准标签及长程红队对话附录，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Language models have been adopted faster than evaluation practices that reliably assess their risks, and a growing share of use is affective rather than informational. Within that traffic users disclose suicide and self-harm (SSH) crises. Existing safety benchmarks score models primarily on refusal, but refusal is not the desired behavior here; the conversation requires a substantive supportive response. We introduce DistressBench, 718 English conversations with clinician-authored user turns spanning 23 SSH subcategories, each paired with a task-specific rubric of weighted atomic criteria across seven dimensions of crisis support and a clinician reference response. A model's score is the weighted average over the criteria its reply satisfies, reading directly as the fraction of required care delivered. The best of 25 frontier models satisfies 88.4% of weighted criteria and the median model 71.4%, against 98.7% for clinician references scored on the same criteria. Pooled across all 25 models, in 35.3% of conversations where a model satisfied every criterion for recognizing the crisis, it failed to route the user toward human help. The shortfall concentrates in the dimensions requiring the model to act rather than withhold. Contrasting each dimension's median model against its best separates capability limits from choices: de-escalation reaches 74.6% in the strongest model against a median of 46.0%, and safety disclaimers 84.6% against 8.3%. Multi-turn conversations score below single-turn ones (68.5% against 74.2%). We release the corpus, rubrics, judge calibration labels, and a companion annex of 20 long-horizon red-team transcripts. These results argue for evaluating crisis safety on the care a model delivers rather than on what it declines to say, and for treating de-escalation and resource routing as deployment requirements.

</details>


### [131] [People Making Conversation: Evaluating How Naturally Speech Models Converse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12802)
*Arya Bastani,Iman Modarressi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出在真实无脚本对话的停顿与话轮结束处评估"是否开口"这一实时语音决策的基准，测试六类系统在时机、静默、打断、内容与长程记忆上的表现并公开全部数据与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话语音模型在每个停顿处都必须决定是否开口，但现有评测只考察边界检测、策略合规或聚合统计，并未直接测评这一决策本身，因此缺乏针对这一核心决策的评测。

Method: 从 Seamless Interaction 的 176 段无脚本对话中抽取 427 个停顿与 482 个话轮结束点，把实时语音系统置于这些位置并给出此前的对话上下文，将其生成结果与真人行为对照评分：停顿处是否开口及多快开口；话轮结束处的延迟、长度与措辞；以及能否回答关于前 30 至 120 秒的问题。评测覆盖六类系统。

Result: 托管 API（GPT Realtime、Gemini 3.1 Flash Live、Grok Voice）几乎在每个停顿处都会回应，无论使用何种端点检测器，仅其延迟（基于静音的端点检测约 1–4 秒，语义话轮检测约 8 秒）决定话语是否落在停顿内。全双工模型（PersonaPlex、Moshi）是唯一经常保持沉默、也是唯一能在人类反应时间内打断的系统。托管系统回复比真人更长。以文本形式提供历史时，托管系统在 Claude Opus 5.5 评判下回忆 41–60% 的对话事实，严格词匹配下为 19–26%；以音频形式听取时，全双工模型回忆 17–22% 和 12–15%。

Conclusion: 该工作将"是否开口"确立为可测量的评测目标，揭示托管 API 与全双工模型在静默、打断时机和长程记忆上的显著差异，并公开标签、评测点、框架及全部输出以支持后续研究。

Abstract: A conversational speech model has to decide, at every pause, whether to speak. Existing evaluations test boundary detection, policy adherence, or aggregate statistics, not this decision. We place real-time speech systems at 427 pauses and 482 turn ends from 176 unscripted conversations in Seamless Interaction, give each the conversation so far, and score what it generates against what the person did: whether and how soon it speaks at a pause; its latency, length, and wording at a turn end; and whether it can answer a question about the preceding 30 to 120 seconds. Across six systems, the hosted APIs (GPT Realtime, Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, Grok Voice) respond at almost every pause whichever endpointer they use; only their latency, about 1 to 4 s with silence-based endpointers and about 8 s with semantic turn detection, decides whether the words land inside the pause. The full-duplex models (PersonaPlex, Moshi) are the only systems that regularly stay silent and the only ones that interrupt within a human reaction time. Hosted systems reply longer than the person. Given the history as text, hosted systems recall 41 to 60% of conversation facts under a Claude Opus 5.5 judge and 19 to 26% under a strict word matcher; hearing it as audio, full-duplex models recall 17 to 22% and 12 to 15%. We release the labels, points, harness, and every output.

</details>


### [132] [When Charts Deceive Web Agents: A Paired Benchmark for Misleading Visualization-Grounded Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12823)
*Yisheng Li,Dongming Han,Hongkun Pan,Bingcheng Mao,Zijian Wang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MisVisAgentBench，首个面向 Web Agent 的成对可执行基准，用于评估误导性可视化对智能体执行的影响；实验显示换用误导性图表会使任务成功率平均下降逾 34 个百分点，且失败集中于预设的误导分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web Agent 日益代替用户浏览、解读并操作网页内容，但其消费的视觉证据并非中立：图表与仪表盘本身可能具有误导性，且不包含任何显式恶意指令。与图表问答不同，Web Agent 将图表视为中间证据，误读会沿执行链传播为具体下游动作，构成尚未被充分研究的可靠性风险。

Method: 构建 MisVisAgentBench：包含 280 个任务实例，以仅在图表证据上存在差异的"干净—误导"匹配对形式组织，支持成对可执行评测。同时提出诊断框架，将误导引发的失败与基线任务难度相剥离，并刻画执行层面的效应（如额外浏览步数与决策犹豫）。

Result: 在 13 个视觉语言 Web Agent 上，用误导性图表替换干净图表后，平均任务成功率下降超过 34 个百分点；失败主要沿预设的误导动作分支发生，表明欺骗性图表是系统性地重定向而非随机干扰智能体决策。

Conclusion: 可视化鲁棒性是 Web Agent 安全与可靠性中关键但被低估的维度，误导性图表可稳定地改变智能体的下游行为，需作为独立评测方向加以关注。

Abstract: Web agents increasingly browse, interpret, and act on web content on behalf of users. In many realistic workflows, however, the visual evidence they consume is not neutral: charts and dashboards can be misleading, shaping judgments without issuing any explicit malicious instruction. Unlike chart question answering, Web Agents treat charts as intermediate evidence whose misinterpretation can propagate into concrete downstream actions --- a reliability risk that remains underexplored. We introduce MisVisAgentBench, the first paired executable benchmark for evaluating how misleading visualizations affect Web Agent execution. It comprises 280 task instances in matched clean--misleading pairs that differ only in chart evidence. We further provide a diagnostic framework that isolates misleading-induced failures from baseline task difficulty (Zhu et al., 2025) and captures execution-level effects such as extra browsing steps and decision hesitation. Across thirteen vision-language Web Agents, replacing clean charts with misleading counterparts reduces the average task success rate by more than 34 percentage points. Moreover, these failures predominantly follow the targeted misleading action branch, suggesting that deceptive charts systematically redirect rather than randomly disrupt agent decisions. These results reveal that visualization robustness is a critical yet understudied dimension of Web Agent safety and reliability.

</details>


### [133] [COFFEQA: Counterfactual-Guided Few-Shot Distillation for LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12837)
*Kavinda Kehelella,Sanghamitra Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出COFFEQA，一种面向开放书问答的反事实引导少样本蒸馏策略，通过构造并验证事实-反事实样本对来约束师生决策边界偏差，在有限样本预算下显著提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏通常数据密集且计算昂贵，尤其在生成任务中。少样本蒸馏虽可缓解该问题，但其性能严重依赖训练样本的选择，靠近教师决策边界的不确定样本会显著损害蒸馏效果，因此需要更有效的样本利用策略。

Method: 提出COFFEQA策略：在生成式问答中定义反事实为最小修改的上下文，促使教师模型将答案改为替代答案；利用事实-反事实样本对提供额外监督，并从几何角度分析该类样本对如何约束师生决策边界差异；同时提出新指标Context Following Difficulty（CFD），基于与预期替代答案的一致性和对修改上下文的依赖性对反事实进行验证，最终用经CFD验证的事实-反事实对进行少样本蒸馏。

Result: 在T5和Qwen2.5系列模型上，跨越5个域内和3个域外问答基准的实验表明，在相同少样本预算下COFFEQA优于仅用事实样本的蒸馏基线。例如在T5设定下，使用32个少样本对时，COFFEQA在TriviaQA上相较事实-only基线平均提升8.77个EM点和9.98个F1点。

Conclusion: 反事实引导的少样本蒸馏能够有效克服样本选择敏感性问题，通过CFD验证的事实-反事实对提供更优监督信号，为资源受限环境下的生成式问答蒸馏提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to train a smaller student model to mimic a larger teacher model for resource-constrained environments. However, the process of distillation is typically data-intensive and computationally expensive, particularly for generative tasks. Few-shot distillation offers an alternative by transferring teacher knowledge using limited training samples. However, we observe that few-shot distillation performance is heavily dependent on the choice of these training samples. In fact, we demonstrate that using more uncertain samples near the teacher's decision boundary can significantly deteriorate distillation performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose Counterfactual-guided Few-shot Distillation in Open-book Question Answering (COFFEQA), a novel strategy that aptly uses counterfactuals to provide additional supervision around challenging factual samples. In the generative QA setting, we define a counterfactual as a minimally modified context that induces the teacher to change its response to an alternative answer. We provide geometric insights on how factual-counterfactual pairs can constrain the deviation between the teacher's and student's decision boundaries. We further introduce a new metric, Context Following Difficulty (CFD) that further validates counterfactuals prior to distillation based on agreement with the intended alternative answer and dependence on the modified context. COFFEQA uses CFD-validated factual-counterfactual pairs for few-shot distillation. Experiments across five in-domain and three out-of-domain QA benchmarks using T5 and Qwen2.5 families show that COFFEQA outperforms factual-only distillation baselines under the same few-shot budget. For instance, on TriviaQA, COFFEQA improves over the factual-only baseline by 8.77 average EM points and 9.98 average F1 points under the T5 setting with 32 few-shot pairs.

</details>


### [134] [Fear Is Not Always Fear: Variation in Emotion Label Equivalence Across Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12841)
*Emily Öhman,Anna Koufakou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 同名情感标签在不同语料间并非可互换的测量目标：跨语料迁移造成严重判别力损失，校准只能修正量纲差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感分类器常被跨数据源使用，前提假设是 fear、surprise 等同名标签在不同资源中含义一致。本文检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 在 GoEmotions、XED、BRIGHTER 共享的五个标签上，用相同输入比较模型并做跨语料迁移；共进行 30 组资源-标签比较，分析训练标签流行率、拟合截距、排序一致性与校准效果。

Result: 同一批文本上，两个 fear 分类器的阳性率分别为 71% 与 8%，但排序基本一致；训练标签流行率可解释约一半的尺度差异，拟合截距可消除大部分。迁移模型总体损失 55% 的原生判别力；排序一致性与该损失仅弱相关；surprise 上各资源对哪些文本表达该情绪判断不一致。在两个外部推文集合上，BRIGHTER 与 XED 训练的分类器给出相反结论（即使经秩归一化，置信区间仍双向排除零）。

Conclusion: 共享的标签名称不足以确立可互换的测量目标，训练资源的选择会改变研究结论。

Abstract: Emotion classifiers are routinely applied beyond their training data, on the assumption that same-named labels such as fear or surprise mean the same thing across resources. We test this assumption for five labels shared by GoEmotions, XED, and BRIGHTER, comparing models on identical inputs and under cross-corpus transfer. On the same texts, one fear classifier flags 71% as fearful and another 8%, yet the two largely agree on how the texts rank. Across 30 resource-label comparisons, training-label prevalence explains about half of this kind of scale mismatch, and a fitted intercept removes most of it. What recalibration cannot repair is larger: transferred models lose 55% of native discrimination in aggregate, agreement in ranking between classifiers is only weakly related to that loss, and for surprise the resources disagree about which texts express the emotion. The choice of training resource can therefore change conclusions: on two external tweet collections, BRIGHTER- and XED-trained classifiers give opposite answers about which shows more surprise, even after rank normalization, with intervals excluding zero in both directions. A shared emotion label name does not establish an interchangeable measurement target.

</details>


### [135] [Unreliable Narrator Framing as a Cost-effective Mechanism for Satire Explanation of Australian and American Satirical News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12863)
*Kanay Gupta,David Y. Liu,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文初步探索了针对澳大利亚讽刺新闻的解释生成，并与美国来源进行对比，提出以"不可靠叙述者"理论为框架、利用开源权重模型生成解释，作为前沿模型的一种可行且低成本的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讽刺新闻是一种特殊的虚假信息：发布方本意是幽默，但读者可能误读。生成解释需要融入近期和本地语境，因此难度较高；同时前沿模型成本昂贵。

Method: 试点研究，从澳大利亚与美国来源收集讽刺新闻文章，基于"不可靠叙述者"理论构建解释生成框架，并使用开源权重模型（open-weights models）生成解释。

Result: 开源权重模型在生成讽刺解释方面可媲美前沿模型且更具成本效益；模型在美国来源上识别讽刺修辞手法的准确率高于澳大利亚来源。

Conclusion: 基于不可靠叙述者理论、由开源权重模型驱动的解释生成是可行的低成本方案；研究贡献了新的数据集和讽刺解释评估基准；跨地域（澳/美）表现存在差异，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Satirical news is a unique form of misinformation: it is intended to be humorous by the original publication source but can be misinterpreted. Explanation of satirical news articles is challenging because recent and local context needs to be incorporated. This is a pilot study that explores explanation generation for satirical news articles from Australia and compares them alongside those from USA. The paper demonstrates that framing explanations on unreliable narrator theory on open-weights models can be a feasible, cost-effective alternative to frontier models. We find that models identify the satirical tropes more accurately on the American source than the Australian one. This work results in a novel dataset and an evaluation benchmark for satirical explanation.

</details>


### [136] [Time Delay System for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12864)
*Youshan Zhang,Jialu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于非线性时滞系统的新型序列建模框架，通过语义相似度动态估计有效时延，实现内容感知的历史召回，兼顾可解释性、O(1)复杂度与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型缺乏显式且可解释的内容感知时序召回机制：LSTM将所有信息压缩进单一隐藏状态而丢失具体历史事件信息；Transformer以二次代价对所有token无差别注意力；Mamba等状态空间模型将历史压缩为固定维度状态，缺乏显式的内容可寻址记忆。

Method: 以非线性时滞系统为基础，有效时延由当前输入与历史输入的语义相似度动态估计。包含三项创新：(i) 可微语义滞后估计器，在历史上计算内容感知的软注意力权重，输出加权历史上下文与相似度分数；(ii) 对角状态空间并具闭式累积和求解，达到每步O(1)复杂度且无需串行循环；(iii) 可解释模糊语义调制机制，学习在即时处理与历史召回之间平衡的上下文相关规则，规则中心对应典型语义上下文。

Result: 在文本分类与生成任务上，实验结果优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过内容感知的动态时延建模缓解了现有序列模型在可解释历史召回方面的缺陷，兼具线性复杂度与可解释性，并在分类和生成任务上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Existing sequence models share a critical limitation: they lack an explicit, interpretable mechanism for content-aware temporal recall. LSTMs compress everything into a single hidden state, thereby losing information about specific past events. Transformers attend to all tokens indiscriminately at quadratic cost, while state-space models like Mamba compress history into a fixed-dimensional state without explicit content-addressable memory.
